## Page Routes & Layout Analysis

**14 total pages. Organized by section:**

- `/app/pages/collections/[slug].vue` — Layout: default (sidebar included); collection detail, items grid, edit/delete forms; sidebar **dead weight**

**User Profile**

**Publish**

- `/app/pages/publish/new.vue` — Middleware: require-auth + require-onboard; Layout: default (sidebar included); upload wizard form; sidebar **dead weight**
- `/app/pages/publish/[id]/edit.vue` — Middleware: require-auth + require-onboard; Layout: default (sidebar included); edit draft wizard; sidebar **dead weight**

- `/app/pages/sign-up.vue` — Layout: home (no sidebar); centered signup form
- `/app/pages/onboard.vue` — Layout: home (no sidebar); department selector
- `/app/pages/cli/auth.vue` — Middleware: require-auth; Layout: home (no sidebar); device authorization code entry

**Special Views**

- **default layout** (8 pages): extensions/*, collections/*, profile, publish/* — all render sidebar but only extensions/index truly needs it

## Nuxt 4 Layout Structure Report

### Overall Architecture
The project uses a **sticky header + flexible sidebar + main content** pattern. Three main layouts exist:

1. **`/app/layouts/default.vue`** — Primary layout with sidebar navigation
2. **`/app/layouts/home.vue`** — No sidebar, header only  
3. **`/app/layouts/mcp-panorama.vue`** — Sidebar delegated to page-level (for specialized visualization)

### Layout Structure (default.vue)

**Wrapper Structure:**
```
h-screen flex flex-col
├─ header (sticky h-16, z-10)
│  └─ TopBar component
└─ div (max-w-7xl mx-auto w-full flex)
   ├─ aside (overflow-hidden w-[240px] or w-0)
   │  └─ Sidebar component
   └─ main (flex-1 overflow-y-auto)
      └─ <slot />
```

**Header:** Full viewport width, `h-16` (64px), sticky, `z-10`, padded `px-5 gap-4`, background `bg-(--color-card)`.

**Content wrapper:** `max-w-7xl` (896px in Tailwind v4) centered with `mx-auto`. Sidebar animates: `w-[240px]` (expanded) or `w-0` (collapsed) with `transition-[width] duration-200`.

### TopBar Component
**Location:** `/app/components/layout/TopBar.vue`

**Structure** (uses `flex` with `contents` wrapper):
- Menu toggle button (hamburger, conditional if `!noSidebar`)
- Logo/link ("AgentCenter", serif font, lg, tracking-tight)
- Search form: `flex-1 max-w-xl mx-auto` (centered, ~330px max)
  - Input: `w-full h-9 pl-9 pr-3 rounded-md`
  - Search icon embedded left, muted color
  - Triggers route push to `/extensions` with `q` query param
- Nav bar (hidden on mobile, `hidden md:flex`):
  - "Explore" dropdown (PopoverContent `w-[220px]`): skills, MCP servers, slash commands, plugins, MCP Panorama
  - Links: Collections, Publish, Docs (disabled)
- Theme toggle, locale switch, user button (right side)

### Sidebar Component
**Location:** `/app/components/layout/Sidebar.vue`

**Renders only when expanded** (`v-if="!collapsed"`):
- Scrollable: `overflow-y-auto px-5 py-3`, hides scrollbars (`[scrollbar-width:none]`)
- **Browse section:** 4 category links (skills, MCP, slash commands, plugins)
  - Active: `border-l-(--color-ink) bg-(--color-sidebar)/60 font-semibold`
- **Categories section:** Hierarchical taxonomy (3 levels: funcCat → l1 → l2)
  - Expandable with chevron icons
  - Colored indicators (small squares, opacity varies on active state)
  - Text sizes: 15px (level 1), 13.5px (level 2), 13px (level 3)

**All pages:** Sidebar appears on `/extensions` routes and derivatives (filter-based browsing).

### Container & Spacing
- **Tailwind max-w:** `max-w-7xl` = 896px (Tailwind v4)
- **TopBar search:** `max-w-xl` = 320px
- **Explore popover:** `w-[220px]`
- **Header height:** `h-16` (64px)
- **Sidebar width:** `w-[240px]` (collapsed: `w-0`)
- **Main content padding:** Nested in `max-w-7xl` container; individual pages add `px-6` (24px each side)

### Key CSS Custom Properties
Theme system uses oklch color tokens: `--color-bg`, `--color-card`, `--color-border`, `--color-ink`, `--color-accent`, etc. Supports light, dark, and mono modes.
```

## Summary

Based on my exploration of the Nuxt 4 codebase, here's where the Browse menu, Categories sidebar, and filter system live:

### 1. **Browse Menu**
**File**: `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/components/layout/Sidebar.vue` (lines 60-75)

Renders a static list of 4 items driven by `BROWSE_ITEMS` constant:
- **Skills** (Zap icon)
- **MCP Servers** (Globe icon)
- **Slash Commands** (Command icon)
- **Plugins** (Plug icon)

Each links to `/extensions?category=<key>`, toggling selection via URL query params.

### 2. **Categories Section**
**File**: Same file, `/app/components/layout/Sidebar.vue` (lines 79-131)

Uses `FUNC_TAXONOMY` from `~/shared/taxonomy` (imported, not fetched). Renders a 3-level hierarchical taxonomy:
- **Level 1**: Functional categories (e.g. "Code", "Data", "Cloud") — expandable with colored square indicator
- **Level 2** (subCat): Subcategories under each L1 — expandable with colored left rule
- **Level 3** (l2): Leaf tags under each L2 — non-expandable

All static, driven by URL query params (`funcCat`, `subCat`, `l2`). No data fetching.

### 3. **Sidebar Mount**
**File**: `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/layouts/default.vue`

Mounted in a collapsible `<aside>` (width transitions: 240px ↔ 0px). Toggleable via TopBar button. Appears on **all pages using the default layout** (most pages except home and mcp-panorama).

### 4. **Pages Where Sidebar Appears**
- **Always visible**: `/extensions` (browse), `/extensions/[slug]` (detail), `/profile`, `/publish`, `/collections`, etc. — any page NOT explicitly setting `layout: "home"` or `layout: "mcp-panorama"`
- **Never visible**: `/` (home — uses `layout: "home"`), `/mcp-panorama` (uses custom `layout: "mcp-panorama"`)

### 5. **Pill-Rail Filter System (CLAUDE.md #3)**
**File**: `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/components/filters/FilterBar.vue`

Single-row pill rail composed of:
- `ScopePills` — All/Personal/Org/Enterprise (inline bordered pills)
- `DeptPicker` — Department dropdown
- `CreatorPicker` — Creator dropdown (if creators facet available)
- `PublisherPicker` — Publisher dropdown (if publishers facet available)
- `TagDrawer` — Tag selection trigger (if tags facet available)

**Only appears on**: `/extensions` index page. Does NOT appear on detail pages, profile, or publish pages. No overlap with sidebar — sidebar filters by browse category + taxonomy; the pill rail filters results within that category.
```

> AGENT

I've gathered enough to see the picture. Let me confirm what I found and ask a few design questions before writing the plan.

**Current state, summarized**

- **Layout:** `app/layouts/default.vue` has a full-width sticky header (`<TopBar>`) but the content below is constrained to `max-w-7xl mx-auto`. So the navbar's logo/user-button sit at viewport edges while the sidebar + main content are centered — that's the misalignment you're seeing.
- **Duplicate Browse:** the sidebar's "Browse" section (Skills / MCPs / Slash Commands / Plugins) **already duplicates** the TopBar's "Explore" dropdown. Same items, two places.
- **Search:** the topbar search just redirects to `/extensions?q=…`. On `/extensions` itself there's already a FilterBar, so the topbar search is mostly redundant.
- **Sidebar waste:** the categories sidebar renders on **8 pages** but is only useful on `/extensions` (index). On detail pages, profile, publish wizards, and collections it's dead weight.

So your four observations are well-founded. Before I write the plan, a few design choices to nail down.

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/.claude/plans/now-let-s-improve-the-structured-whale.md",
  "content": "# Layout redesign: align navbar, simplify sidebar, prune search\n\n## Context\n\nThe current top-level layout has four problems the user surfaced (all valid):\n\n1. **Navbar doesn't align with content.** `app/layouts/default.vue` puts `<TopBar>` at full viewport width (header is sticky and unconstrained), but the row below is wrapped in `max-w-7xl mx-auto`. The logo/user-button sit at viewport edges while the sidebar + main content are centered, so nothing lines up vertically.\n2. **Browse menu is duplicated.** `Sidebar.vue` renders a \"Browse\" section (Skills / MCP / Slash / Plugins) that is identical to the TopBar's \"Explore\" dropdown — same four links, two homes.\n3. **Topbar search is dead weight.** It just pushes to `/extensions?q=…`. On `/extensions` the FilterBar already exposes search/filter; on every other page the topbar search competes for attention without adding capability.\n4. **Categories sidebar shows everywhere.** The categories taxonomy renders on all 8 default-layout pages (extensions list, extension detail, collections, profile, publish wizards). Only `/extensions` (index) uses it — on the others it's visual noise.\n\nGoal: a navbar that lines up with content, primary categories promoted to the navbar as inline links, search removed, and the categories sidebar scoped to `/extensions` only.\n\n## Design decisions (confirmed)\n\n- **Nav style:** inline links — `Skills · MCPs · Slash Commands · Plugins · Collections · Publish` (replacing the Explore dropdown).\n- **Sidebar scope:** only `/extensions` (index). Detail page, profile, publish, collections, auth — no sidebar.\n- **Search:** removed entirely. Search lives on `/extensions` via the existing FilterBar.\n\n## Target layout architecture\n\nThree layouts, with clear responsibilities:\n\n| Layout | Header | Sidebar | Used by |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `default.vue` | yes (max-w-7xl inner) | no | `/`, `/extensions/[slug]`, `/collections/*`, `/profile`, `/publish/*`, `/sign-in`, `/sign-up`, `/onboard`, `/cli/auth` |\n| `browse.vue` (new) | yes (max-w-7xl inner) | yes (Categories only) | `/extensions` index |\n| `mcp-panorama.vue` | custom | custom | `/mcp-panorama` |\n\n`home.vue` is merged into `default.vue` (both become header + slot). Pages currently declaring `layout: 'home'` drop the meta and inherit `default`.\n\n## Implementation\n\n### 1. `app/components/layout/TopBar.vue` — restructure\n\n- Wrap the existing inner flex row in `<div class=\"max-w-7xl mx-auto w-full flex items-center gap-4 px-5\">` so the navbar's logical content aligns with the page container.\n- Keep the outer `<header>` full-width (background colour, border still spans the viewport — only the *content* is constrained).\n- **Remove** the search `<form>` block (the `flex-1 max-w-xl mx-auto` search input).\n- **Remove** the \"Explore\" Popover dropdown (`Skills`, `MCP Servers`, `Slash Commands`, `Plugins`, `MCP Panorama` items).\n- **Replace** with inline links rendered as a flex row: Skills, MCPs, Slash Commands, Plugins, Collections, Publish — each linking to `/extensions?category=<key>` for the first four; `/collections` and `/publish` for the last two. Reuse the existing `NuxtLink` + active-state classes already present in the Explore popover items.\n- **Remove** the hamburger menu toggle (`!noSidebar` block) — sidebar toggling belongs to `browse.vue`, not the global header.\n- **MCP Panorama**: dropped from primary nav; remains reachable from the MCPs index. If reachability matters later, surface it as a quiet link on the MCPs filter page.\n- Theme toggle / locale switcher / user button stay on the right.\n\nActive-state styling: keep the current Editorial-Ivory \"active = bold + left rule or underline, no tinted fill\" pattern (per CLAUDE.md §11 — accent reserved for primary CTAs, not nav).\n\n### 2. `app/layouts/default.vue` — drop the sidebar\n\nReplace the current body with:\n\n```vue\n<div class=\"h-screen flex flex-col\">\n  <header class=\"sticky top-0 z-10 h-16 bg-(--color-card) border-b\">\n    <TopBar :no-sidebar=\"true\" />\n  </header>\n  <main class=\"flex-1 overflow-y-auto\">\n    <div class=\"max-w-7xl mx-auto w-full px-5\">\n      <slot />\n    </div>\n  </main>\n</div>\n```\n\n- Remove the `<aside>` + transition width logic.\n- Remove the sidebar-collapse state (lift it to `browse.vue` or drop entirely).\n\n### 3. `app/layouts/browse.vue` — new layout (sidebar + main)\n\nMirrors the old `default.vue` structure, but the sidebar is local to this layout. Toggle button lives inside the sidebar (or browse layout) rather than the global header.\n\n```vue\n<div class=\"h-screen flex flex-col\">\n  <header class=\"sticky top-0 z-10 h-16 bg-(--color-card) border-b\">\n    <TopBar :no-sidebar=\"false\" />\n  </header>\n  <div class=\"max-w-7xl mx-auto w-full flex flex-1 overflow-hidden\">\n    <aside :class=\"['shrink-0 transition-[width] duration-200', collapsed ? 'w-0' : 'w-[240px]']\">\n      <Sidebar v-if=\"!collapsed\" @toggle=\"collapsed = !collapsed\" />\n    </aside>\n    <main class=\"flex-1 overflow-y-auto\">\n      <slot />\n    </main>\n  </div>\n</div>\n```\n\nThe collapse state moves here (via `useState('browse-sidebar-collapsed', () => false)` so it persists across navigations within the layout). TopBar's `no-sidebar` prop hides the toggle button on non-browse pages; on browse pages the toggle moves into the sidebar's header (a small chevron).\n\n### 4. `app/layouts/home.vue` — delete (merged into default)\n\nAfter merging, remove `home.vue` and strip `definePageMeta({ layout: 'home' })` from the five pages that use it:\n\n- `app/pages/index.vue`\n- `app/pages/sign-in.vue`\n- `app/pages/sign-up.vue`\n- `app/pages/onboard.vue`\n- `app/pages/cli/auth.vue`\n\n(Default layout now matches what `home.vue` used to do.)\n\n### 5. `app/components/layout/Sidebar.vue` — strip Browse section\n\n- Delete the `BROWSE_ITEMS` block (lines ~60–75) — those links now live in the navbar.\n- Keep the \"Categories\" hierarchy (`FUNC_TAXONOMY`, 3-level expand/collapse).\n- Add a small collapse chevron at the top of the sidebar (emits `toggle` to `browse.vue`).\n\n### 6. `app/pages/extensions/index.vue` — opt into browse layout\n\nAdd `definePageMeta({ layout: 'browse' })` at the top.\n\n### 7. No changes required to\n\n- `app/components/filters/FilterBar.vue` (pill rail) — independent of the sidebar.\n- `app/pages/extensions/[slug].vue` — gains usable horizontal space now that the sidebar is gone.\n- `app/pages/mcp-panorama.vue` — its custom layout is untouched.\n- Routing, i18n prefixes, FTS search — none of these touch the layout layer.\n\n## Files changed\n\n| File | Change |\n|---|---|\n| `app/components/layout/TopBar.vue` | rewrite: inner max-w wrapper, inline nav, drop search & Explore dropdown, drop hamburger when `no-sidebar` |\n| `app/layouts/default.vue` | rewrite: remove sidebar |\n| `app/layouts/browse.vue` | **new**: header + sidebar + main |\n| `app/layouts/home.vue` | **delete** |\n| `app/components/layout/Sidebar.vue` | remove Browse section; keep Categories; add internal collapse trigger |\n| `app/pages/extensions/index.vue` | add `definePageMeta({ layout: 'browse' })` |\n| `app/pages/index.vue` | remove `layout: 'home'` meta |\n| `app/pages/sign-in.vue` | remove `layout: 'home'` meta |\n| `app/pages/sign-up.vue` | remove `layout: 'home'` meta |\n| `app/pages/onboard.vue` | remove `layout: 'home'` meta |\n| `app/pages/cli/auth.vue` | remove `layout: 'home'` meta |\n\n## Verification\n\n1. **Visual alignment.** Run `bun dev`, visit `/` — confirm the logo and user button sit at the same horizontal positions as the page content beneath them (both inside `max-w-7xl mx-auto`).\n2. **Nav links.** Click each of Skills / MCPs / Slash Commands / Plugins from the navbar → lands on `/extensions?category=<key>` with the right facet applied. Active state shows on the link matching the current category param.\n3. **Sidebar scope.** Visit `/extensions` → sidebar visible, Categories only (no Browse section). Visit `/extensions/[slug]`, `/collections`, `/profile`, `/publish` → no sidebar; content fills the `max-w-7xl` width.\n4. **Sidebar collapse.** On `/extensions`, click the new collapse chevron → sidebar slides to `w-0`; refresh → state persists within session.\n5. **Auth/onboard pages.** `/sign-in`, `/sign-up`, `/onboard`, `/cli/auth` render the same as before (default layout now matches old home layout).\n6. **No regressions.** `bun run validate` (lint + typecheck + tests) — all green. Manually exercise the home page hero, extensions filter pill rail, and a publish wizard step to confirm no layout assumptions broke.\n7. **i18n.** Switch locale to `/zh/` and repeat steps 2–3 — links and active states still resolve correctly under the prefixed routes.\n\n## Out of scope (deliberate)\n\n- Redesigning the pill-rail FilterBar (per CLAUDE.md §3 the single-row rail is a locked decision).\n- A command-palette / cmd-K search replacement — search may return later as an icon trigger, but not in this change.\n- Mobile-specific drawer treatment for the sidebar. Current `w-0` collapse works on small screens; a real drawer/sheet can ship separately.\n- MCP Panorama navigation reshuffle beyond removing it from the Explore dropdown.\n"
}

{
  "plan": "# Layout redesign: align navbar, simplify sidebar, prune search\n\n## Context\n\nThe current top-level layout has four problems the user surfaced (all valid):\n\n1. **Navbar doesn't align with content.** `app/layouts/default.vue` puts `<TopBar>` at full viewport width (header is sticky and unconstrained), but the row below is wrapped in `max-w-7xl mx-auto`. The logo/user-button sit at viewport edges while the sidebar + main content are centered, so nothing lines up vertically.\n2. **Browse menu is duplicated.** `Sidebar.vue` renders a \"Browse\" section (Skills / MCP / Slash / Plugins) that is identical to the TopBar's \"Explore\" dropdown — same four links, two homes.\n3. **Topbar search is dead weight.** It just pushes to `/extensions?q=…`. On `/extensions` the FilterBar already exposes search/filter; on every other page the topbar search competes for attention without adding capability.\n4. **Categories sidebar shows everywhere.** The categories taxonomy renders on all 8 default-layout pages (extensions list, extension detail, collections, profile, publish wizards). Only `/extensions` (index) uses it — on the others it's visual noise.\n\nGoal: a navbar that lines up with content, primary categories promoted to the navbar as inline links, search removed, and the categories sidebar scoped to `/extensions` only.\n\n## Design decisions (confirmed)\n\n- **Nav style:** inline links — `Skills · MCPs · Slash Commands · Plugins · Collections · Publish` (replacing the Explore dropdown).\n- **Sidebar scope:** only `/extensions` (index). Detail page, profile, publish, collections, auth — no sidebar.\n- **Search:** removed entirely. Search lives on `/extensions` via the existing FilterBar.\n\n## Target layout architecture\n\nThree layouts, with clear responsibilities:\n\n| Layout | Header | Sidebar | Used by |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `default.vue` | yes (max-w-7xl inner) | no | `/`, `/extensions/[slug]`, `/collections/*`, `/profile`, `/publish/*`, `/sign-in`, `/sign-up`, `/onboard`, `/cli/auth` |\n| `browse.vue` (new) | yes (max-w-7xl inner) | yes (Categories only) | `/extensions` index |\n| `mcp-panorama.vue` | custom | custom | `/mcp-panorama` |\n\n`home.vue` is merged into `default.vue` (both become header + slot). Pages currently declaring `layout: 'home'` drop the meta and inherit `default`.\n\n## Implementation\n\n### 1. `app/components/layout/TopBar.vue` — restructure\n\n- Wrap the existing inner flex row in `<div class=\"max-w-7xl mx-auto w-full flex items-center gap-4 px-5\">` so the navbar's logical content aligns with the page container.\n- Keep the outer `<header>` full-width (background colour, border still spans the viewport — only the *content* is constrained).\n- **Remove** the search `<form>` block (the `flex-1 max-w-xl mx-auto` search input).\n- **Remove** the \"Explore\" Popover dropdown (`Skills`, `MCP Servers`, `Slash Commands`, `Plugins`, `MCP Panorama` items).\n- **Replace** with inline links rendered as a flex row: Skills, MCPs, Slash Commands, Plugins, Collections, Publish — each linking to `/extensions?category=<key>` for the first four; `/collections` and `/publish` for the last two. Reuse the existing `NuxtLink` + active-state classes already present in the Explore popover items.\n- **Remove** the hamburger menu toggle (`!noSidebar` block) — sidebar toggling belongs to `browse.vue`, not the global header.\n- **MCP Panorama**: dropped from primary nav; remains reachable from the MCPs index. If reachability matters later, surface it as a quiet link on the MCPs filter page.\n- Theme toggle / locale switcher / user button stay on the right.\n\nActive-state styling: keep the current Editorial-Ivory \"active = bold + left rule or underline, no tinted fill\" pattern (per CLAUDE.md §11 — accent reserved for primary CTAs, not nav).\n\n### 2. `app/layouts/default.vue` — drop the sidebar\n\nReplace the current body with:\n\n```vue\n<div class=\"h-screen flex flex-col\">\n  <header class=\"sticky top-0 z-10 h-16 bg-(--color-card) border-b\">\n    <TopBar :no-sidebar=\"true\" />\n  </header>\n  <main class=\"flex-1 overflow-y-auto\">\n    <div class=\"max-w-7xl mx-auto w-full px-5\">\n      <slot />\n    </div>\n  </main>\n</div>\n```\n\n- Remove the `<aside>` + transition width logic.\n- Remove the sidebar-collapse state (lift it to `browse.vue` or drop entirely).\n\n### 3. `app/layouts/browse.vue` — new layout (sidebar + main)\n\nMirrors the old `default.vue` structure, but the sidebar is local to this layout. Toggle button lives inside the sidebar (or browse layout) rather than the global header.\n\n```vue\n<div class=\"h-screen flex flex-col\">\n  <header class=\"sticky top-0 z-10 h-16 bg-(--color-card) border-b\">\n    <TopBar :no-sidebar=\"false\" />\n  </header>\n  <div class=\"max-w-7xl mx-auto w-full flex flex-1 overflow-hidden\">\n    <aside :class=\"['shrink-0 transition-[width] duration-200', collapsed ? 'w-0' : 'w-[240px]']\">\n      <Sidebar v-if=\"!collapsed\" @toggle=\"collapsed = !collapsed\" />\n    </aside>\n    <main class=\"flex-1 overflow-y-auto\">\n      <slot />\n    </main>\n  </div>\n</div>\n```\n\nThe collapse state moves here (via `useState('browse-sidebar-collapsed', () => false)` so it persists across navigations within the layout). TopBar's `no-sidebar` prop hides the toggle button on non-browse pages; on browse pages the toggle moves into the sidebar's header (a small chevron).\n\n### 4. `app/layouts/home.vue` — delete (merged into default)\n\nAfter merging, remove `home.vue` and strip `definePageMeta({ layout: 'home' })` from the five pages that use it:\n\n- `app/pages/index.vue`\n- `app/pages/sign-in.vue`\n- `app/pages/sign-up.vue`\n- `app/pages/onboard.vue`\n- `app/pages/cli/auth.vue`\n\n(Default layout now matches what `home.vue` used to do.)\n\n### 5. `app/components/layout/Sidebar.vue` — strip Browse section\n\n- Delete the `BROWSE_ITEMS` block (lines ~60–75) — those links now live in the navbar.\n- Keep the \"Categories\" hierarchy (`FUNC_TAXONOMY`, 3-level expand/collapse).\n- Add a small collapse chevron at the top of the sidebar (emits `toggle` to `browse.vue`).\n\n### 6. `app/pages/extensions/index.vue` — opt into browse layout\n\nAdd `definePageMeta({ layout: 'browse' })` at the top.\n\n### 7. ## Approved Plan:
# Layout redesign: align navbar, simplify sidebar, prune search

## Context

The current top-level layout has four problems the user surfaced (all valid):

1. **Navbar doesn't align with content.** `app/layouts/default.vue` puts `<TopBar>` at full viewport width (header is sticky and unconstrained), but the row below is wrapped in `max-w-7xl mx-auto`. The logo/user-button sit at viewport edges while the sidebar + main content are centered, so nothing lines up vertically.
2. **Browse menu is duplicated.** `Sidebar.vue` renders a "Browse" section (Skills / MCP / Slash / Plugins) that is identical to the TopBar's "Explore" dropdown — same four links, two homes.
3. **Topbar search is dead weight.** It just pushes to `/extensions?q=…`. On `/extensions` the FilterBar already exposes search/filter; on every other page the topbar search competes for attention without adding capability.
4. **Categories sidebar shows everywhere.** The categories taxonomy renders on all 8 default-layout pages (extensions list, extension detail, collections, profile, publish wizards). Only `/extensions` (index) uses it — on the others it's visual noise.

Goal: a navbar that lines up with content, primary categories promoted to the navbar as inline links, search removed, and the categories sidebar scoped to `/extensions` only.

## Design decisions (confirmed)

- **Nav style:** inline links — `Skills · MCPs · Slash Commands · Plugins · Collections · Publish` (replacing the Explore dropdown).
- **Sidebar scope:** only `/extensions` (index). Detail page, profile, publish, collections, auth — no sidebar.
- **Search:** removed entirely. Search lives on `/extensions` via the existing FilterBar.

## Target layout architecture

Three layouts, with clear responsibilities:

| Layout | Header | Sidebar | Used by |
|---|---|---|---|
| `default.vue` | yes (max-w-7xl inner) | no | `/`, `/extensions/[slug]`, `/collections/*`, `/profile`, `/publish/*`, `/sign-in`, `/sign-up`, `/onboard`, `/cli/auth` |
| `browse.vue` (new) | yes (max-w-7xl inner) | yes (Categories only) | `/extensions` index |
| `mcp-panorama.vue` | custom | custom | `/mcp-panorama` |

`home.vue` is merged into `default.vue` (both become header + slot). Pages currently declaring `layout: 'home'` drop the meta and inherit `default`.

## Implementation

### 1. `app/components/layout/TopBar.vue` — restructure

- Wrap the existing inner flex row in `<div class="max-w-7xl mx-auto w-full flex items-center gap-4 px-5">` so the navbar's logical content aligns with the page container.
- Keep the outer `<header>` full-width (background colour, border still spans the viewport — only the *content* is constrained).
- **Remove** the search `<form>` block (the `flex-1 max-w-xl mx-auto` search input).
- **Remove** the "Explore" Popover dropdown (`Skills`, `MCP Servers`, `Slash Commands`, `Plugins`, `MCP Panorama` items).
- **Replace** with inline links rendered as a flex row: Skills, MCPs, Slash Commands, Plugins, Collections, Publish — each linking to `/extensions?category=<key>` for the first four; `/collections` and `/publish` for the last two. Reuse the existing `NuxtLink` + active-state classes already present in the Explore popover items.
- **Remove** the hamburger menu toggle (`!noSidebar` block) — sidebar toggling belongs to `browse.vue`, not the global header.
- **MCP Panorama**: dropped from primary nav; remains reachable from the MCPs index. If reachability matters later, surface it as a quiet link on the MCPs filter page.
- Theme toggle / locale switcher / user button stay on the right.

Active-state styling: keep the current Editorial-Ivory "active = bold + left rule or underline, no tinted fill" pattern (per CLAUDE.md §11 — accent reserved for primary CTAs, not nav).

### 2. `app/layouts/default.vue` — drop the sidebar

Replace the current body with:

```vue
<div class="h-screen flex flex-col">
  <header class="sticky top-0 z-10 h-16 bg-(--color-card) border-b">
    <TopBar :no-sidebar="true" />
  </header>
  <main class="flex-1 overflow-y-auto">
    <div class="max-w-7xl mx-auto w-full px-5">
      <slot />
    </div>
  </main>
</div>
```

- Remove the `<aside>` + transition width logic.
- Remove the sidebar-collapse state (lift it to `browse.vue` or drop entirely).

### 3. `app/layouts/browse.vue` — new layout (sidebar + main)

Mirrors the old `default.vue` structure, but the sidebar is local to this layout. Toggle button lives inside the sidebar (or browse layout) rather than the global header.

```vue
<div class="h-screen flex flex-col">
  <header class="sticky top-0 z-10 h-16 bg-(--color-card) border-b">
    <TopBar :no-sidebar="false" />
  </header>
  <div class="max-w-7xl mx-auto w-full flex flex-1 overflow-hidden">
    <aside :class="['shrink-0 transition-[width] duration-200', collapsed ? 'w-0' : 'w-[240px]']">
      <Sidebar v-if="!collapsed" @toggle="collapsed = !collapsed" />
    </aside>
    <main class="flex-1 overflow-y-auto">
      <slot />
    </main>
  </div>
</div>
```

The collapse state moves here (via `useState('browse-sidebar-collapsed', () => false)` so it persists across navigations within the layout). TopBar's `no-sidebar` prop hides the toggle button on non-browse pages; on browse pages the toggle moves into the sidebar's header (a small chevron).

### 4. `app/layouts/home.vue` — delete (merged into default)

After merging, remove `home.vue` and strip `definePageMeta({ layout: 'home' })` from the five pages that use it:

- `app/pages/index.vue`
- `app/pages/sign-in.vue`
- `app/pages/sign-up.vue`
- `app/pages/onboard.vue`
- `app/pages/cli/auth.vue`

(Default layout now matches what `home.vue` used to do.)

### 5. `app/components/layout/Sidebar.vue` — strip Browse section

- Delete the `BROWSE_ITEMS` block (lines ~60–75) — those links now live in the navbar.
- Keep the "Categories" hierarchy (`FUNC_TAXONOMY`, 3-level expand/collapse).
- Add a small collapse chevron at the top of the sidebar (emits `toggle` to `browse.vue`).

### 6. `app/pages/extensions/index.vue` — opt into browse layout

Add `definePageMeta({ layout: 'browse' })` at the top.

### 7. No changes required to

- `app/components/filters/FilterBar.vue` (pill rail) — independent of the sidebar.
- `app/pages/extensions/[slug].vue` — gains usable horizontal space now that the sidebar is gone.
- `app/pages/mcp-panorama.vue` — its custom layout is untouched.
- Routing, i18n prefixes, FTS search — none of these touch the layout layer.

## Files changed

| File | Change |
|---|---|
| `app/components/layout/TopBar.vue` | rewrite: inner max-w wrapper, inline nav, drop search & Explore dropdown, drop hamburger when `no-sidebar` |
| `app/layouts/default.vue` | rewrite: remove sidebar |
| `app/layouts/browse.vue` | **new**: header + sidebar + main |
| `app/layouts/home.vue` | **delete** |
| `app/components/layout/Sidebar.vue` | remove Browse section; keep Categories; add internal collapse trigger |
| `app/pages/extensions/index.vue` | add `definePageMeta({ layout: 'browse' })` |
| `app/pages/index.vue` | remove `layout: 'home'` meta |
| `app/pages/sign-in.vue` | remove `layout: 'home'` meta |
| `app/pages/sign-up.vue` | remove `layout: 'home'` meta |
| `app/pages/onboard.vue` | remove `layout: 'home'` meta |
| `app/pages/cli/auth.vue` | remove `layout: 'home'` meta |

## Verification

1. **Visual alignment.** Run `bun dev`, visit `/` — confirm the logo and user button sit at the same horizontal positions as the page content beneath them (both inside `max-w-7xl mx-auto`).
2. **Nav links.** Click each of Skills / MCPs / Slash Commands / Plugins from the navbar → lands on `/extensions?category=<key>` with the right facet applied. Active state shows on the link matching the current category param.
3. **Sidebar scope.** Visit `/extensions` → sidebar visible, Categories only (no Browse section). Visit `/extensions/[slug]`, `/collections`, `/profile`, `/publish` → no sidebar; content fills the `max-w-7xl` width.
4. **Sidebar collapse.** On `/extensions`, click the new collapse chevron → sidebar slides to `w-0`; refresh → state persists within session.
5. **Auth/onboard pages.** `/sign-in`, `/sign-up`, `/onboard`, `/cli/auth` render the same as before (default layout now matches old home layout).
6. **No regressions.** `bun run validate` (lint + typecheck + tests) — all green. Manually exercise the home page hero, extensions filter pill rail, and a publish wizard step to confirm no layout assumptions broke.
7. **i18n.** Switch locale to `/zh/` and repeat steps 2–3 — links and active states still resolve correctly under the prefixed routes.

## Branch, commits, PR

Per `CLAUDE.md`: feature branch (never commit to `main`), Conventional Commits per coherent unit, no `Co-Authored-By:` trailer, no Claude Code footer, no force-push, no `--no-verify`.

**Branch:** `refactor/layout-shell` (or pick a name aligned with current naming on `main`).

**One PR.** The pieces are interdependent — splitting risks intermediate broken states (e.g. dropping the sidebar from `default` before `browse` exists would leave `/extensions` sidebar-less mid-stack). Inside the PR, commits are ordered so the app builds and renders sanely at each step:

1. **`feat(layout): add browse layout for /extensions`** — Create `app/layouts/browse.vue` as a duplicate of the current `default.vue` (header + sidebar + main). Add `definePageMeta({ layout: 'browse' })` to `app/pages/extensions/index.vue`. No visible change yet — `/extensions` now goes through `browse`, every other default-layout page still has the sidebar via `default`.
2. **`refactor(layout): drop sidebar from default layout`** — Strip the `<aside>` + collapse logic from `app/layouts/default.vue`. Visible change: `/extensions/[slug]`, `/collections/*`, `/profile`, `/publish/*` lose the sidebar; `/extensions` keeps it via `browse`. App still compiles cleanly.
3. **`refactor(layout): remove duplicate Browse section from sidebar`** — Delete the `BROWSE_ITEMS` block from `app/components/layout/Sidebar.vue`; add the internal collapse chevron. Sidebar is now Categories-only.
4. **`refactor(topbar): align with content container, inline category nav`** — Wrap TopBar's inner row in `max-w-7xl mx-auto`; remove the Explore dropdown and the search form; add inline links (Skills / MCPs / Slash Commands / Plugins / Collections / Publish); drop the global hamburger toggle. This is the largest single commit — keep it self-contained.
5. **`refactor(layout): merge home into default`** — Delete `app/layouts/home.vue`; remove `definePageMeta({ layout: 'home' })` from `index.vue`, `sign-in.vue`, `sign-up.vue`, `onboard.vue`, `cli/auth.vue`.

If commit 4 grows unwieldy on review, it can be split as `topbar: align with content container` + `topbar: replace Explore dropdown with inline category nav` + `topbar: remove search` — three commits that each pass `bun run validate` independently.

**Scope token:** I've used `layout` and `topbar` above. If `docs/plan.md` defines a current phase scope (e.g. `p7-polish`), substitute that — match whatever the most recent commits on `main` are using.

**Validate cadence:** Run `bun run validate` per-commit if cheap, otherwise at minimum at PR boundary (as `docs/plan.md` §13 prescribes the split).

**PR description** should:
- Lead with the four user-surfaced problems and a one-line fix per problem.
- Include a before/after screenshot of the navbar alignment + the `/extensions/[slug]` page reclaiming the full width.
- Note the layout count change (3 → 3, but with reshuffled responsibilities) and which routes moved between layouts.
- No "🤖 Generated with Claude Code" footer, no `Co-Authored-By:` trailer.

**Do not push** until the user explicitly asks. Open the PR with `gh pr create` only after they confirm the local branch is review-ready.

## Out of scope (deliberate)

- Redesigning the pill-rail FilterBar (per CLAUDE.md §3 the single-row rail is a locked decision).
- A command-palette / cmd-K search replacement — search may return later as an icon trigger, but not in this change.
- Mobile-specific drawer treatment for the sidebar. Current `w-0` collapse works on small screens; a real drawer/sheet can ship separately.
- MCP Panorama navigation reshuffle beyond removing it from the Explore dropdown.

52	const PANORAMA_ITEMS: { key: string; to: string; labelKey: string; Icon: Component }[] = [
53	  { key: "mcp-panorama", to: "/mcp-panorama", labelKey: "nav.mcpPanorama", Icon: Map },
54	]

56	const exploreOpen = ref(false)
57	
58	// Explore is "active" whenever the user is on the extensions index or
59	// any of the panorama routes that live under the dropdown. The dropdown
60	// itself is type-first navigation — users must pick a category to enter
61	// /extensions — but `/extensions` (no filter) is still reachable as a
62	// fallback, so the trigger should light up there too.
63	const localeExtensionsPath = computed(() => localePath("/extensions"))
64	const localePanoramaPaths = computed(() =>
65	  PANORAMA_ITEMS.map((p) => localePath(p.to)),
66	)
67	const exploreActive = computed(() => {
68	  if (route.path === localeExtensionsPath.value) return true
69	  if (route.path.startsWith(`${localeExtensionsPath.value}/`)) return true
70	  return localePanoramaPaths.value.some(
71	    (p) => route.path === p || route.path.startsWith(`${p}/`),
72	  )
73	})
74	</script>

18	
19	type Category = "skills" | "mcp" | "slash" | "plugins"
20	
21	const BROWSE_ITEMS: { key: Category; labelKey: string; Icon: Component }[] = [
22	  { key: "skills", labelKey: "sidebar.skills", Icon: Zap },
23	  { key: "mcp", labelKey: "sidebar.mcpServers", Icon: Globe2 },
24	  { key: "slash", labelKey: "sidebar.slashCommands", Icon: Command },
25	  { key: "plugins", labelKey: "sidebar.plugins", Icon: Plug },
26	]
27	
28	const activeCategory = computed<Category | null>(() => {
29	  const raw = route.query.category
30	  if (typeof raw === "string" && ["skills", "mcp", "slash", "plugins"].includes(raw)) {
31	    return raw as Category
32	  }
33	  return null
34	})
35	
36	const activeFuncCat = computed(() => (typeof route.query.funcCat === "string" ? route.query.funcCat : null))
37	const activeSubCat = computed(() => (typeof route.query.subCat === "string" ? route.query.subCat : null))
38	const activeL2 = computed(() => (typeof route.query.l2 === "string" ? route.query.l2 : null))
39	
40	function buildHref(updates: Record<string, string | null>): string {
41	  const params = new URLSearchParams()
42	  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(route.query)) {
43	    if (typeof v === "string" && k !== "page") params.set(k, v)
44	  }
45	  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(updates)) {
46	    if (v === null) params.delete(k)
47	    else params.set(k, v)
48	  }
49	  const qs = params.toString()
50	  const path = localePath("/extensions")
51	  return qs ? `${path}?${qs}` : path
52	}
53	</script>

  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/components/layout/Sidebar.vue",
  "content": "<script setup lang=\"ts\">\nimport { ChevronDown, ChevronRight } from \"lucide-vue-next\"\nimport { FUNC_CAT_COLORS, FUNC_TAXONOMY } from \"~~/shared/taxonomy\"\n\ndefineProps<{ collapsed: boolean }>()\n\nconst { t } = useI18n()\nconst route = useRoute()\nconst localePath = useLocalePath()\n\nconst activeFuncCat = computed(() => (typeof route.query.funcCat === \"string\" ? route.query.funcCat : null))\nconst activeSubCat = computed(() => (typeof route.query.subCat === \"string\" ? route.query.subCat : null))\nconst activeL2 = computed(() => (typeof route.query.l2 === \"string\" ? route.query.l2 : null))\n\nfunction buildHref(updates: Record<string, string | null>): string {\n  const params = new URLSearchParams()\n  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(route.query)) {\n    if (typeof v === \"string\" && k !== \"page\") params.set(k, v)\n  }\n  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(updates)) {\n    if (v === null) params.delete(k)\n    else params.set(k, v)\n  }\n  const qs = params.toString()\n  const path = localePath(\"/extensions\")\n  return qs ? `${path}?${qs}` : path\n}\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <div\n    v-if=\"!collapsed\"\n    class=\"min-w-[200px] flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-5 py-3 text-(--color-ink) [scrollbar-width:none] [&::-webkit-scrollbar]:hidden\"\n  >\n    <h2 class=\"px-2 pb-1.5 font-serif text-[12px] italic tracking-wide text-(--color-ink-muted)\">\n      {{ t(\"sidebar.categories\") }}\n    </h2>\n    <div class=\"flex flex-col gap-px\">\n      <div v-for=\"cat in FUNC_TAXONOMY\" :key=\"cat.key\">\n        <NuxtLink\n          :to=\"buildHref({ funcCat: activeFuncCat === cat.key && !activeSubCat && !activeL2 ? null : cat.key, subCat: null, l2: null })\"\n          class=\"flex w-full items-center gap-2.5 rounded-md px-2 py-1.5 text-left text-[15px] font-semibold transition hover:bg-(--color-card)\"\n          :class=\"activeFuncCat === cat.key && !activeSubCat && !activeL2 ? 'bg-(--color-sidebar)/60 text-(--color-ink)' : 'text-(--color-ink)'\"\n          :aria-expanded=\"activeFuncCat === cat.key\"\n        >\n          <span class=\"size-[9px] shrink-0 rounded-sm\" :style=\"{ background: FUNC_CAT_COLORS[cat.key] }\" />\n          <span class=\"flex-1\">{{ t(`taxonomy.funcCat.${cat.key}`) }}</span>\n          <ChevronDown v-if=\"activeFuncCat === cat.key\" :size=\"14\" class=\"shrink-0 text-(--color-ink-muted)\" aria-hidden=\"true\" />\n          <ChevronRight v-else :size=\"14\" class=\"shrink-0 text-(--color-ink-muted)\" aria-hidden=\"true\" />\n        </NuxtLink>\n\n        <div v-if=\"activeFuncCat === cat.key\">\n          <div v-for=\"l1 in cat.l1\" :key=\"l1.key\" class=\"ml-2\">\n            <NuxtLink\n              :to=\"buildHref({ funcCat: cat.key, subCat: activeSubCat === l1.key ? null : l1.key, l2: null })\"\n              class=\"flex w-full items-center gap-2.5 rounded-md px-2 py-1.5 text-left text-[13.5px] font-medium hover:bg-(--color-card)\"\n              :class=\"activeSubCat === l1.key && !activeL2 ? 'bg-(--color-sidebar)/60 text-(--color-ink) font-semibold' : 'text-(--color-ink)'\"\n              :aria-expanded=\"l1.l2.length > 0 ? activeSubCat === l1.key : undefined\"\n            >\n              <span\n                class=\"w-[1.5px] shrink-0 self-stretch rounded\"\n                :style=\"{ background: FUNC_CAT_COLORS[cat.key], opacity: activeSubCat === l1.key && !activeL2 ? 1 : 0.35 }\"\n              />\n              <span class=\"flex-1\">{{ t(`taxonomy.l1.${l1.key}`) }}</span>\n              <template v-if=\"l1.l2.length > 0\">\n                <ChevronDown v-if=\"activeSubCat === l1.key\" :size=\"13\" class=\"shrink-0 text-(--color-ink-muted)\" aria-hidden=\"true\" />\n                <ChevronRight v-else :size=\"13\" class=\"shrink-0 text-(--color-ink-muted)\" aria-hidden=\"true\" />\n              </template>\n            </NuxtLink>\n\n            <div v-if=\"activeSubCat === l1.key\">\n              <NuxtLink\n                v-for=\"l2key in l1.l2\"\n                :key=\"l2key\"\n                :to=\"buildHref({ funcCat: cat.key, subCat: l1.key, l2: activeL2 === l2key ? null : l2key })\"\n                class=\"flex w-full items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md py-1 pr-2 pl-6 text-left text-[13px] hover:bg-(--color-card)\"\n                :class=\"activeL2 === l2key ? 'bg-(--color-sidebar)/60 text-(--color-ink) font-semibold' : 'text-(--color-ink)/85'\"\n              >\n                <span class=\"bg-(--color-ink-muted) size-[3px] shrink-0 rounded-full\" />\n                {{ t(`taxonomy.l2.${l2key}`) }}\n              </NuxtLink>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  </div>\n</template>\n"

  "content": "// @vitest-environment nuxt\nimport { describe, it, expect } from \"vitest\"\nimport { mountSuspended } from \"@nuxt/test-utils/runtime\"\nimport { defineComponent } from \"vue\"\nimport Sidebar from \"./Sidebar.vue\"\n\nconst NuxtLinkStub = defineComponent({\n  name: \"NuxtLink\",\n  props: { to: { type: String, required: true } },\n  template: \"<a :href=\\\"to\\\"><slot /></a>\",\n})\n\nconst mountOpts = {\n  props: { collapsed: false },\n  global: { stubs: { NuxtLink: NuxtLinkStub } },\n}\n\ndescribe(\"Sidebar\", () => {\n  it(\"renders a single Categories section header\", async () => {\n    const wrapper = await mountSuspended(Sidebar, mountOpts)\n    const html = wrapper.html().toLowerCase()\n    expect(html).toContain(\"categor\")\n    // Browse moved to the navbar — only Categories remains as an h2.\n    const h2s = wrapper.findAll(\"h2\")\n    expect(h2s.length).toBe(1)\n  })\n\n  it(\"no longer renders a Browse section (type pills moved to TopBar)\", async () => {\n    const wrapper = await mountSuspended(Sidebar, mountOpts)\n    const text = wrapper.text().toLowerCase()\n    expect(text).not.toContain(\"browse\")\n    // No more category=skills/mcp/slash/plugins links in the sidebar.\n    const categoryLinks = wrapper\n      .findAll(\"a\")\n      .filter((a) => /[?&]category=/.test(a.attributes(\"href\") ?? \"\"))\n    expect(categoryLinks.length).toBe(0)\n  })\n\n  it(\"does not render a primary-nav 'Explore' section (that moved to TopBar)\", async () => {\n    const wrapper = await mountSuspended(Sidebar, mountOpts)\n    expect(wrapper.text().toLowerCase()).not.toContain(\"explore\")\n  })\n\n  it(\"does not render the old Collections placeholder section\", async () => {\n    const wrapper = await mountSuspended(Sidebar, mountOpts)\n    const html = wrapper.html().toLowerCase()\n    expect(html).not.toContain(\"my tools\")\n    expect(html).not.toContain(\"new group\")\n  })\n\n  it(\"renders all three funcCat L0 labels collapsed by default\", async () => {\n    const wrapper = await mountSuspended(Sidebar, mountOpts)\n    const html = wrapper.html().toLowerCase()\n    expect(html).toContain(\"work task\")\n    expect(html).toContain(\"business\")\n    expect(html).toContain(\"tools\")\n  })\n\n  it(\"does not expand any funcCat by default (L1 rows hidden until clicked)\", async () => {\n    const wrapper = await mountSuspended(Sidebar, mountOpts)\n    const html = wrapper.html().toLowerCase()\n    expect(html).not.toContain(\"system design\")\n    expect(html).not.toContain(\"network protocols\")\n  })\n\n  it(\"collapsed prop hides the inner content\", async () => {\n    const wrapper = await mountSuspended(Sidebar, {\n      ...mountOpts,\n      props: { collapsed: true },\n    })\n    expect(wrapper.findAll(\"a\").length).toBe(0)\n  })\n})\n"
}

  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/components/layout/TopBar.vue",
  "content": "<script setup lang=\"ts\">\nimport { Menu } from \"lucide-vue-next\"\n\nwithDefaults(defineProps<{ collapsed: boolean; noSidebar?: boolean }>(), {\n  noSidebar: false,\n})\nconst emit = defineEmits<{ \"toggle-sidebar\": [] }>()\n\nconst { t } = useI18n()\nconst route = useRoute()\nconst localePath = useLocalePath()\n\ntype CategoryKey = \"skills\" | \"mcp\" | \"slash\" | \"plugins\"\n\nconst CATEGORY_ITEMS: { key: CategoryKey; labelKey: string }[] = [\n  { key: \"skills\", labelKey: \"sidebar.skills\" },\n  { key: \"mcp\", labelKey: \"sidebar.mcpServers\" },\n  { key: \"slash\", labelKey: \"sidebar.slashCommands\" },\n  { key: \"plugins\", labelKey: \"sidebar.plugins\" },\n]\n\nconst localeExtensionsPath = computed(() => localePath(\"/extensions\"))\n\nfunction isCategoryActive(key: CategoryKey): boolean {\n  if (route.path !== localeExtensionsPath.value) return false\n  return route.query.category === key\n}\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <div class=\"mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl h-full flex items-center gap-4 px-5\">\n    <button\n      v-if=\"!noSidebar\"\n      type=\"button\"\n      class=\"p-2 rounded hover:bg-(--color-sidebar) focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-(--color-accent)\"\n      :aria-label=\"t('nav.toggleSidebar')\"\n      :aria-expanded=\"!collapsed\"\n      @click=\"emit('toggle-sidebar')\"\n    >\n      <Menu :size=\"20\" aria-hidden=\"true\" />\n    </button>\n\n    <NuxtLink\n      :to=\"localePath('/')\"\n      class=\"font-serif text-lg tracking-tight hover:opacity-80\"\n    >\n      AgentCenter\n    </NuxtLink>\n\n    <nav\n      class=\"hidden md:flex items-center gap-1 text-sm flex-1\"\n      :aria-label=\"t('nav.primary')\"\n    >\n      <NuxtLink\n        v-for=\"item in CATEGORY_ITEMS\"\n        :key=\"item.key\"\n        :to=\"{ path: localeExtensionsPath, query: { category: item.key } }\"\n        class=\"px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-(--color-ink-muted) hover:text-(--color-ink) hover:bg-(--color-sidebar)\"\n        :class=\"isCategoryActive(item.key) ? 'text-(--color-ink) font-semibold bg-(--color-sidebar)/60' : ''\"\n      >\n        {{ t(item.labelKey) }}\n      </NuxtLink>\n      <NuxtLink\n        :to=\"localePath('/collections')\"\n        class=\"px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-(--color-ink-muted) hover:text-(--color-ink) hover:bg-(--color-sidebar)\"\n        active-class=\"text-(--color-ink) font-semibold bg-(--color-sidebar)/60\"\n      >\n        {{ t(\"nav.collections\") }}\n      </NuxtLink>\n      <NuxtLink\n        :to=\"localePath('/publish')\"\n        class=\"px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-(--color-ink-muted) hover:text-(--color-ink) hover:bg-(--color-sidebar)\"\n        active-class=\"text-(--color-ink) font-semibold bg-(--color-sidebar)/60\"\n      >\n        {{ t(\"nav.publish\") }}\n      </NuxtLink>\n    </nav>\n\n    <ThemeSwitch />\n    <LocaleSwitch />\n    <UserButton />\n  </div>\n</template>\n"
}

The Explore Popover is replaced with four\n   inline links: Skills, MCPs, Slash Commands, Plugins. Panorama remains directly addressable; if it\n   needs a discovery surface later it can live on the MCPs page.\n\ni18n: nav.explore → nav.primary (aria-label for the inline nav).\nEOF\n)\"",

```
[{"body":"## Summary\n\nAdds `scripts/seed-editorial-collections.ts` — a write-only counterpart to `scripts/seed.ts` for the five editorial public collections defined in `shared/data/collections.ts`.\n\n`scripts/seed.ts` TRUNCATEs `organizations`, `tags`, and `collections` with CASCADE, which is fine locally as a \"reset to demo state\" but catastrophic in prod (every published extension, every uploaded file, every real user's saved rows would be wiped). This script is the safe path:\n\n- Halts up-front if any owner user (`user-amy`, …) or referenced extension (`ext-1`, `ext-3`, …) is missing — no half-applied state.\n- Inserts each collection only when its slug is free; existing rows are left untouched (no metadata overwrite, no visibility flip).\n- Inserts `collection_items` with `onConflictDoNothing` on the composite PK so re-runs after adding a new `extensionId` to `COLLECTIONS` pick it up and zero-change re-runs are no-ops.\n- Never truncates anything, never touches other tables.\n\nDeliberately not added to `vercel-build` — even though this script is idempotent today, `vercel-build` is for strictly-infrastructural operations on every push. \"Ensure these five specific editorial curations exist\" is editorial content, not infrastructure.\n\n## Test plan\n\n- [x] Local smoke test against the dev DB (which already has the 5 collections from a prior `bun run db:seed`): reports `created 0, kept 5, inserted 0 item(s)` — true idempotent no-op.\n- [x] Lint + typecheck green.\n- [ ] First real invocation against prod is gated on `user-amy`/`user-ben`/… existing in prod (currently they don't, since `db:seed` has never run there); the script will halt with a \"missing prerequisites\" message and not insert anything. Path forward TBD in a follow-up.\n\n## Invocation\n\n```bash\nvercel env pull --environment=production .env.prod.real\nDATABASE_URL=\"$(grep ^DATABASE_URL .env.prod.real | cut -d= -f2- | tr -d '\"')\" \\\n  bun scripts/seed-editorial-collections.ts\nrm .env.prod.real\n```\n\n<!-- This is an auto-generated comment: release notes by coderabbit.ai -->\n\n## Summary by CodeRabbit\n\n* **Chores**\n  * Added database seeding script to initialize editorial collections in the system with safeguards against data loss and validation checks.\n\n<!-- review_stack_entry_start -->\n\n[![Review Change Stack](https://storage.googleapis.com/coderabbit_public_assets/review-stack-in-coderabbit-ui.svg)](https://app.coderabbit.ai/change-stack/ainaive/agentcenter-nuxt/pull/31?utm_source=github_walkthrough&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=change_stack)\n\n<!-- review_stack_entry_end -->\n\n<!-- end of auto-generated comment: release notes by coderabbit.ai -->","number":31,"title":"chore(scripts): add idempotent editorial-collections insert for prod"},{"body":"## Summary\n\nTurns the existing implicit-bookmark schema (the `systemKind='saved'` collection) into a real \"save and organize\" surface: users create named collections (bilingual name + description), add/remove extensions, and toggle each collection between **private** and **public**. Public collections get a stable, copy-pasteable `/collections/<shortcode>` URL and appear on a `/collections` browse page.\n\n- The bookmark button on extension pages opens a checkbox-list popover (Saved pinned at top, user collections below, inline \"+ New collection\") instead of a single toggle.\n- Profile gains a \"Collections\" tab next to \"Saved\".\n- TopBar gains a \"Collections\" nav entry.\n- Web scope only — `/api/v1` is **untouched** so the CLI keeps working unchanged. CLI install-collection is deferred per the plan.\n\n## Schema\n\n`shared/db/schema/collection.ts` gains `slug` (10-char base36, unique), `description`/`descriptionZh`, `visibility` enum (`private` | `public`), `publishedAt` (stamped on first private→public flip), `updatedAt`, plus indexes on `ownerUserId` and `(visibility, publishedAt DESC)`.\n\nMigration `drizzle/0007_wakeful_cloak.sql` was hand-edited to add `slug` nullable → backfill via `md5(id||rand())` → flip to NOT NULL + UNIQUE so the upgrade is safe on existing rows.\n\n## API surface (internal)\n\n| Route | Purpose |\n| --- | --- |\n| `GET /api/internal/collections` | Owner list; lazy-seeds Saved |\n| `POST /api/internal/collections` | Create |\n| `GET /api/internal/collections/[slug]` | Public to anyone, private to owner only |\n| `POST /api/internal/collections/[slug]/update` | Rename / edit copy / visibility toggle |\n| `POST /api/internal/collections/[slug]/delete` | Owner-only; system collections return 422 |\n| `POST /api/internal/collections/[slug]/items` | Add extension (idempotent) |\n| `POST /api/internal/collections/[slug]/items/[extensionId]/remove` | Remove |\n| `GET /api/internal/collections/public` | Paginated browse |\n| `GET /api/internal/collection-memberships?extensionId=…` | Powers the popover checkbox state |\n\n`/api/v1` is **unchanged**.\n\n## Pivotal decisions\n\n1. **Two-state visibility** (`private` | `public`). Unlisted deferred — the enum can grow without a migration if needed.\n2. **`/collections/<shortcode>`** URLs, same path for public + private; visitors hitting a private one get a 404 (no info leak vs. truly missing).\n3. **POST-based mutations** instead of PATCH/DELETE on `[slug]` — matches the codebase's existing `publish/submit.post` convention and sidesteps a Nitro InternalApi type collision that broke `$fetch` method typing.\n4. **System \"Saved\" stays.** The picker pins it at the top; the existing recordInstall flow still writes to it via the shared `getOrCreateSystem` helper (now also returning the slug it generated).\n\n## Test plan\n\nEnd-to-end was already verified against a running dev server:\n\n- [x] `bun run db:migrate` against the existing local DB — clean, journal updated.\n- [x] Sign-up → list collections (lazy-seeded Saved row appears) → create a public collection → add `ext-1` → owner GET shows `itemCount:1`.\n- [x] Anonymous GET on the public collection returns 200; after `update {visibility:\"private\"}` returns 404.\n- [x] `publishedAt` stays populated across visibility toggles (stable browse sort).\n- [x] `POST .../[saved-slug]/delete` returns 422 with `system_collection_locked`.\n- [x] `/en/collections` and `/en/collections/[slug]` SSR-render correctly.\n- [x] `/zh/collections/[slug]` shows bilingual `nameZh` + Chinese visibility chip.\n- [x] `bun run validate` (lint + typecheck + 242/242 tests) green.\n\nStill to do post-merge:\n- [ ] Browser-level smoke test (popover click, \"+ New collection\", visibility toggle, copy-link affordance).\n- [ ] Add `server/utils/queries/collections.test.ts` and `shared/validators/collection.test.ts` integration coverage.\n- [ ] Decide whether to fold the existing \"Saved\" tab into the new \"Collections\" tab in a follow-up.\n\n## Out of scope (deferred)\n\n- No `/api/v1/collections/*` public registry endpoints. CLI cannot install-by-collection yet.\n- No org/department-scoped collections (only user-owned). `ownerOrgId` can be added later.\n- No featured/curated collections on the home page.\n- No fork, rating, or comments on collections.\n\n<!-- This is an auto-generated comment: release notes by coderabbit.ai -->\n\n## Summary by CodeRabbit\n\n## Release Notes\n\n* **New Features**\n  * **Collections**: Create and organize personalized collections of extensions\n  * Save extensions to collections via a new popover interface\n  * Browse and discover public collections from the community\n  * Manage collection visibility (private/public) and share with others\n  * Full multilingual support including English and Chinese localization\n\n<!-- review_stack_entry_start -->\n\n[![Review Change Stack](https://storage.googleapis.com/coderabbit_public_assets/review-stack-in-coderabbit-ui.svg)](https://app.coderabbit.ai/change-stack/ainaive/agentcenter-nuxt/pull/30?utm_source=github_walkthrough&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=change_stack)\n\n<!-- review_stack_entry_end -->\n\n<!-- end of auto-generated comment: release notes by coderabbit.ai -->","number":30,"title":"feat(collections): user-curated collections with public sharing"},{"body":"## Summary\n\n- Home page now uses a new sidebar-less layout (`app/layouts/home.vue`); the category Sidebar duplicated the home discovery rail.\n- On every other page, the `(sidebar + main)` row in `app/layouts/default.vue` is wrapped in `mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl` so the sidebar reads as the left column of a centered content frame instead of a viewport-edge rail.\n- `TopBar` gains an optional `noSidebar` prop that hides the hamburger toggle on layouts without a sidebar.\n\n## Test plan\n\n- [ ] `/` and `/zh` render TopBar + `HomeHero` + `HomeDiscovery` with no left sidebar column and no hamburger toggle.\n- [ ] `/extensions` on a wide monitor (≥1440 px): sidebar + main share one centered max-w-7xl frame with equal gutters either side.\n- [ ] TopBar hamburger on `/extensions` still collapses the sidebar; main expands inside the centered frame.\n- [ ] `/extensions/[slug]` detail layout composes correctly inside the centered frame.\n- [ ] Narrow viewport (~1024 px): no horizontal scroll, max-w-7xl becomes a no-op.\n- [ ] `/mcp-panorama` unchanged.\n- [ ] `/sign-in`, `/sign-up`, `/onboard`, `/cli/auth` unchanged (still on `default.vue` with sidebar).\n- [ ] `bun run validate` clean.\n\n<!-- This is an auto-generated comment: release notes by coderabbit.ai -->\n\n## Summary by CodeRabbit\n\n* **New Features**\n  * Home page layout redesigned with centered content and maximum-width constraints\n  * Sticky header navigation bar added to home page\n  * Skip-to-content accessibility link implemented\n\n* **Improvements**\n  * Default layout inner wrapper now constrained and centered for improved content presentation\n\n<!-- review_stack_entry_start -->\n\n[![Review Change Stack](https://storage.googleapis.com/coderabbit_public_assets/review-stack-in-coderabbit-ui.svg)](https://app.coderabbit.ai/change-stack/ainaive/agentcenter-nuxt/pull/29?utm_source=github_walkthrough&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=change_stack)\n\n<!-- review_stack_entry_end -->\n\n<!-- end of auto-generated comment: release notes by coderabbit.ai -->","number":29,"title":"feat(layout): drop sidebar on home, center sidebar+main elsewhere"}]

1	# Layout redesign: align navbar, simplify sidebar, prune search
2	
3	## Context
4	
5	The current top-level layout has four problems the user surfaced (all valid):
6	
7	1. **Navbar doesn't align with content.** `app/layouts/default.vue` puts `<TopBar>` at full viewport width (header is sticky and unconstrained), but the row below is wrapped in `max-w-7xl mx-auto`. The logo/user-button sit at viewport edges while the sidebar + main content are centered, so nothing lines up vertically.
8	2. **Browse menu is duplicated.** `Sidebar.vue` renders a "Browse" section (Skills / MCP / Slash / Plugins) that is identical to the TopBar's "Explore" dropdown — same four links, two homes.
9	3. **Topbar search is dead weight.** It just pushes to `/extensions?q=…`. On `/extensions` the FilterBar already exposes search/filter; on every other page the topbar search competes for attention without adding capability.
10	4. **Categories sidebar shows everywhere.** The categories taxonomy renders on all 8 default-layout pages (extensions list, extension detail, collections, profile, publish wizards). Only `/extensions` (index) uses it — on the others it's visual noise.
11	
12	Goal: a navbar that lines up with content, primary categories promoted to the navbar as inline links, search removed, and the categories sidebar scoped to `/extensions` only.
13	
14	## Design decisions (confirmed)
15	
16	- **Nav style:** inline links — `Skills · MCPs · Slash Commands · Plugins · Collections · Publish` (replacing the Explore dropdown).
17	- **Sidebar scope:** only `/extensions` (index). Detail page, profile, publish, collections, auth — no sidebar.
18	- **Search:** removed entirely. Search lives on `/extensions` via the existing FilterBar.
19	
20	## Target layout architecture
21	
22	Three layouts, with clear responsibilities:
23	
24	| Layout | Header | Sidebar | Used by |
25	|---|---|---|---|
26	| `default.vue` | yes (max-w-7xl inner) | no | `/`, `/extensions/[slug]`, `/collections/*`, `/profile`, `/publish/*`, `/sign-in`, `/sign-up`, `/onboard`, `/cli/auth` |
27	| `browse.vue` (new) | yes (max-w-7xl inner) | yes (Categories only) | `/extensions` index |
28	| `mcp-panorama.vue` | custom | custom | `/mcp-panorama` |
29	
30	`home.vue` is merged into `default.vue` (both become header + slot). Pages currently declaring `layout: 'home'` drop the meta and inherit `default`.
```

## Analysis Complete

### File Structure Overview

**Main page:** `/app/pages/extensions/index.vue`

### 1. Top-of-page Header
The page renders a `<h1>` with `extensions.browseTitle` i18n key:
```vue
<h1 class="font-serif text-3xl tracking-tight text-(--color-ink)">
  {{ t("extensions.browseTitle") }}
</h1>
```
**Text:** "Browse all"

### 2. FilterBar Component Structure
**Location:** `/app/components/filters/FilterBar.vue`

Renders 5 child filter controls in this order:

1. **ScopePills** — `/app/components/filters/ScopePills.vue` — Filters by scope (all, personal, org, enterprise); renders 4 radio-button pills
2. **DeptPicker** — `/app/components/filters/DeptPicker.vue` — Filters by department; popover with hierarchical tree navigation, shows "My Dept", "Org", "All" quick-select pills
3. **CreatorPicker** — `/app/components/filters/CreatorPicker.vue` (if creators exist) — Filters by single creator; popover dropdown with "Any" + list of creators with counts
4. **PublisherPicker** — `/app/components/filters/PublisherPicker.vue` (if publishers exist) — Filters by single publisher; popover dropdown with "Any" + publisher list with i18n support (nameZh for Chinese locale)
5. **TagDrawer** — `/app/components/filters/TagDrawer.vue` (if tags exist) — Multi-select tag filter with "any/all" matching mode toggle

### 3. TagDrawer Component Details
**Location:** `/app/components/filters/TagDrawer.vue`

Behavior:
- Controlled open/close toggle via button click
- Multi-select checkboxes for tags (up to 14 visible, "show more" expander)
- Match mode toggle: "any" (OR) or "all" (AND) for active tags
- Clear all tags button
- Each tag shows count and can be individually toggled
- Badge on toggle button displays active count

### 4. Other UI Controls
**ResultsSummary** — `/app/components/extension/ResultsSummary.vue` — Displays:
- Results count ("Showing X results")
- Active filter chips with dismiss buttons
- "Clear all filters" button
- **SortSelect** — `/app/components/filters/SortSelect.vue` (right-aligned)

**ExtGrid/ExtGridSkeleton** — Shows grid of extension cards or loading skeleton

### 5. Text Strings Found
From `/i18n/locales/en.json`:
- `"browseTitle": "Browse all"` — Main heading
- `"browse": "Browse marketplace"` — Elsewhere in app
- `"backToBrowse": "Browse extensions"` — Navigation link text

---

**File paths:**
- `/app/pages/extensions/index.vue`
- `/app/components/filters/FilterBar.vue`
- `/app/components/filters/ScopePills.vue`, `DeptPicker.vue`, `CreatorPicker.vue`, `PublisherPicker.vue`, `TagDrawer.vue`
- `/app/components/extension/ResultsSummary.vue`
- `/app/components/extension/ExtGrid.vue`
- `/i18n/locales/en.json` (i18n strings)

Perfect. I have all the information needed. Here's my comprehensive findings:

## Analysis Summary

### 1. Sidebar Current Render (Confirmed)
The Sidebar only displays the Categories taxonomy (funcCat 3-level hierarchy). After refactor, it correctly shows:
- **Top level**: 3 funcCat keys colored distinctly (workTask, business, tools)
- **L1 level**: 3 sub-items per funcCat (e.g., systemDesign, softDev, testing under workTask)
- **L2 level**: 3 items per L1 (e.g., reqAnalysis, funcDesign, archDesign under systemDesign)

File: `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/components/layout/Sidebar.vue`

### 2. FUNC_TAXONOMY Structure
File: `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/shared/taxonomy.ts`

```typescript
export const FUNC_TAXONOMY: FuncTaxonomyNode[] = [
  { key: "workTask", l1: [
    { key: "systemDesign", l2: ["reqAnalysis", "funcDesign", "archDesign"] },
    { key: "softDev", l2: ["frontend", "backend", "devops"] },
    { key: "testing", l2: ["unitTest", "intTest", "perfTest"] }
  ]},
  { key: "business", l1: [/* 3 items */] },
  { key: "tools", l1: [/* 3 items */] }
]
```
- **Top-level funcCat entries**: 3 (workTask, business, tools)
- **L1 keys per funcCat**: 3 each = 9 total
- **L2 keys**: 3 per L1 = 27 total

### 3. "All" / Clear State
**Current limitation**: There is **NO dedicated "All" row**. The sidebar clears funcCat by clicking the same funcCat row again (toggle). The logic is:
```typescript
:to="buildHref({ funcCat: activeFuncCat === cat.key && !activeSubCat && !activeL2 ? null : cat.key, ... })"
```
This works but UX is non-obvious. Reusable pattern from other components exists (see below).

### 4. URL Query Params on Fresh `/extensions` Visit
- **URL params**: funcCat, subCat, l2
- **Fresh visit with no params**: All computed values are null, **no sidebar row is highlighted**
- The sidebar renders all 3 funcCat rows in a "neutral" state (no background highlight, ChevronRight icon shown)
- User can start drilling down immediately or the page shows all extensions unfiltered

### 5. "Clear" / "Reset" Pattern — Already Exists
**Example from CreatorPicker** (`/app/components/filters/CreatorPicker.vue`, lines 51-58):
```typescript
<button @click="selectCreator(undefined)">
  <span>{{ t("filters.creator.any") }}</span>
</button>
```
The pattern is simple: a button that calls `update({ creator: undefined })` or passes `null` to the href builder. This is orthogonal to the sidebar's toggle pattern.

### 6. Tags/Facets Architecture
**Tags are fully orthogonal to funcCat taxonomy.**

- **Tag facets live**: `/api/internal/facets` endpoint (fetched once on page load, not filter-dependent)
- **Tags UI**: TagDrawer component (`/app/components/filters/TagDrawer.vue`)
- **Tag vocabulary**: 30+ predefined labels (search, real-time, stable, beta, python, api, sql, etc.) stored in `/shared/tags.ts`
- **Tag filter mechanism**: Independent array stored in URL as `?tags=a,b,c` with optional `tagMatch=any|all` mode
- **No overlap**: Tags are separate from funcCat/subCat/l2; both can be active simultaneously (e.g., `?funcCat=workTask&tags=python,testing`)

**Tag facets endpoint returns**:
```typescript
type TagFacet = { id: string, count: number }
```

### 7. Dept/Creator/Publisher Pickers — Visual & Structural Analysis

All three use Popover (modal picker) pattern with identical visual weight:

| Component | UI Type | Visual Weight | Design | Max Width |
|-----------|---------|---------------|--------|-----------|
| **DeptPicker** | Popover tree | Medium-high | Hierarchical tree with "My/Org/All" quick tabs | 360px |
| **CreatorPicker** | Popover list | Low | Simple scrollable list with "Any" fallback | 280px |
| **PublisherPicker** | Popover list | Low | Simple scrollable list with "Any" fallback | 280px |

**Key insight**: Dept is the most complex (hierarchical, 3 quick buttons, tree expansion), while Creator/Publisher are lightweight lists. **None would lose functionality in sidebar** (the model is simple: pick one or none), but Dept's tree UI is bulky and its hierarchical navigation would compete visually with the funcCat tree already in the sidebar.

**Current visual hierarchy** (FilterBar, lines 12–18):
```vue
<ScopePills /> <!-- category/scope chips -->
<DeptPicker /> <!-- popover -->
<CreatorPicker /> <!-- popover -->
<PublisherPicker /> <!-- popover -->
<TagDrawer /> <!-- drawer with toggle buttons -->
```

All filters (funcCat, dept, creator, publisher, tags) are **independent, filter-neutral** (no cascading), so any could theoretically move to the sidebar, but the UX consequence is visual competition and Dept's tree depth would add > 20 sidebar rows.

---

**File paths for reference:**
- `/app/components/layout/Sidebar.vue` — sidebar rendering
- `/shared/taxonomy.ts` — FUNC_TAXONOMY definition
- `/app/components/filters/TagDrawer.vue` — tag facets UI
- `/app/components/filters/{DeptPicker,CreatorPicker,PublisherPicker}.vue` — popover pickers
- `/app/components/filters/FilterBar.vue` — layout of all filters
- `/app/composables/useFilters.ts` — filter state logic
- `/shared/validators/filters.ts` — filter URL schema
```

## Approved Plan:
# Extensions page polish: "All" category + search/sort toolbar

## Context

PR #32 (the navbar/sidebar refactor) is open on `refactor/layout-shell`. Two follow-up tweaks the user wants stacked onto the same branch:

1. **Categories has no "All" row.** Today the sidebar clears the funcCat filter by clicking the active funcCat row a second time — the toggle is non-obvious. There's no dedicated affordance for "show all categories" from the sidebar.
2. **The `<h1>Browse all</h1>` heading on `/extensions` is dead weight.** It sits above the FilterBar adding visual mass without giving the user anything to do. Search was removed from the navbar in PR #32 with the rationale that search lives on `/extensions` via the FilterBar — but the FilterBar doesn't actually have a search input today. Replacing the heading with a slim **search-input + sort toolbar** lands that promise and reclaims vertical space.

Decision #3 (single-row pill rail) is **not changing**. Tags / Scope / Dept / Creator / Publisher stay in the rail. The user opted for the conservative split.

## Design decisions (confirmed)

- **Sidebar:** add a single `All` row above the funcCat list. Highlighted when `funcCat`, `subCat`, `l2` are all unset. Clicking it clears those three params.
- **Top of `/extensions`:** drop the `<h1>` heading. Replace with a slim toolbar — search input on the left (full-flex), `SortSelect` on the right. The existing FilterBar (Scope / Dept / Creator / Publisher / TagDrawer) sits below the toolbar, unchanged.
- **Search wiring:** input is bound to `route.query.q`. Debounced ~250ms; pushes to the current route with `q` updated (and `page` cleared). Submit (Enter) flushes immediately. Empty string removes the `q` param.
- **Sort relocation:** `SortSelect` moves out of `ResultsSummary` into the new toolbar. `ResultsSummary` keeps the count + active-chip dismissers + "Clear all".

## Implementation

### 1. `app/components/layout/Sidebar.vue` — add "All" row

Add a single NuxtLink above the funcCat loop:

```vue
<NuxtLink
  :to="buildHref({ funcCat: null, subCat: null, l2: null })"
  class="flex w-full items-center gap-2.5 rounded-md px-2 py-1.5 text-[15px] font-semibold transition hover:bg-(--color-card)"
  :class="!activeFuncCat && !activeSubCat && !activeL2 ? 'bg-(--color-sidebar)/60 text-(--color-ink)' : 'text-(--color-ink)'"
>
  <span class="size-[9px] shrink-0 rounded-sm bg-(--color-ink-muted)" />
  <span class="flex-1">{{ t("sidebar.allCategories") }}</span>
</NuxtLink>
```

Reuses the existing `buildHref()` helper — it already strips/sets params correctly. No new logic. The neutral-grey square (vs. the colored funcCat squares) signals "no filter."

### 2. i18n: add `sidebar.allCategories`

- `i18n/locales/en.json` → `"allCategories": "All"`
- `i18n/locales/zh.json` → `"allCategories": "全部"`

### 3. `app/pages/extensions/index.vue` — toolbar + drop heading

- Remove `<h1>{{ t("extensions.browseTitle") }}</h1>`.
- Insert a new toolbar above `<FilterBar />`:
  - Left: a search `<input>` bound to a local `q` ref initialized from `route.query.q`.
  - Right: `<SortSelect />` (moved here from ResultsSummary).
- Reuse the existing topbar-search styling pattern (the one removed in PR #32) — same Lucide `Search` icon, `h-9 pl-9 pr-3 rounded-md` shape — so the visual language stays consistent.
- Wire the debounce inline (`setTimeout` + `clearTimeout` in a `watch`) — VueUse isn't currently a dependency and adding it for one debounce is overkill. ~10 lines.

Sketch:

```vue
<div class="flex items-center gap-3 pt-4 pb-3">
  <form role="search" class="flex-1 relative" @submit.prevent="flushSearch">
    <Search :size="16" class="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-(--color-ink-muted) pointer-events-none" />
    <input
      v-model="q"
      type="search"
      :placeholder="t('search.placeholder')"
      class="w-full h-9 pl-9 pr-3 rounded-md border border-(--color-border) bg-(--color-bg) text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-(--color-accent)"
    />
  </form>
  <SortSelect />
</div>
```

### 4. `app/components/extension/ResultsSummary.vue` — drop SortSelect

Remove the embedded `<SortSelect />`. Keep the results count, active-filter chips, and "Clear all" button. The component header layout collapses to a single row (count on the left, chips wrap, clear-all on the right).

If any other page imports `ResultsSummary` expecting the sort selector to be there, this is a breaking change — but it's only used from `/extensions/index.vue` (one consumer), confirmed by the previous exploration.

### 5. Keep i18n key reachable but unused (optional cleanup)

`extensions.browseTitle` becomes orphan. Leave it for now — separate i18n cleanup pass.

## Files changed

| File | Change |
|---|---|
| `app/components/layout/Sidebar.vue` | add "All" row above funcCat loop |
| `i18n/locales/en.json` | `+sidebar.allCategories` |
| `i18n/locales/zh.json` | `+sidebar.allCategories` |
| `app/pages/extensions/index.vue` | drop `<h1>`, add search+sort toolbar, local `q` ref, debounced URL push |
| `app/components/extension/ResultsSummary.vue` | remove `<SortSelect />`, layout collapse |

## Branch, commits, push

Stack on top of the existing `refactor/layout-shell` branch (PR #32 is still open). Two new commits, kept independent so each compiles cleanly:

1. **`feat(sidebar): add "All" row to Categories`** — Sidebar + i18n keys.
2. **`feat(extensions): replace 'Browse all' heading with search + sort toolbar`** — Page toolbar, ResultsSummary slim-down.

Per `CLAUDE.md`: no `Co-Authored-By:` trailer, no Claude Code footer, no `--no-verify`. Run `bun run validate` at the end before reporting done.

**Do not push.** The user explicitly asked to keep these commits local for now. PR #32 will pick them up when the user gives the next "push" instruction.

## Verification

1. `bun run validate` — lint + typecheck + 242 tests should stay green. (If a test was asserting the `Browse all` heading text, update it.)
2. `bun dev`, visit `/extensions` — confirm:
   - No heading; toolbar renders with search input filling the row and sort selector on the right.
   - Typing in the search input updates the URL after ~250ms; pressing Enter flushes immediately; clearing the input removes `q`.
   - Sidebar shows `All` highlighted on a fresh `/extensions` visit; clicking a funcCat de-highlights it; clicking `All` clears funcCat/subCat/l2.
3. `/zh/extensions` — same behavior; sidebar shows `全部`.
4. ResultsSummary still shows count + active chips + "Clear all", just without the sort selector.

## Out of scope (deliberate)

- Moving Tags / Scope / Dept / Creator / Publisher into the sidebar. Decision #3 is intact.
- Reshaping the FilterBar itself.
- Cleaning up the now-orphan `extensions.browseTitle` i18n key.
- Mobile-specific toolbar layout (search input stacks above sort on narrow screens by default via `flex-wrap` if needed).

```

1	import {
2	  parseFilters,
3	  searchParamsToInput,
4	  serializeFilters,
5	  type Filters,
6	} from "~~/shared/validators/filters"
7	
8	export type FilterUpdate = Partial<Filters>
9	
10	export function useFilters() {
11	  const route = useRoute()
12	  const router = useRouter()
13	  const localePath = useLocalePath()
14	
15	  const filters = computed<Filters>(() => {
16	    const params = new URLSearchParams()
17	    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(route.query)) {
18	      if (Array.isArray(v)) {
19	        for (const item of v) if (item !== null) params.append(k, String(item))
20	      } else if (v !== null && v !== undefined) {
21	        params.append(k, String(v))
22	      }
23	    }
24	    return parseFilters(searchParamsToInput(params))
25	  })
26	
27	  function update(partial: FilterUpdate) {
28	    const next: FilterUpdate = { ...filters.value, ...partial }
29	    if (!("page" in partial)) next.page = undefined
30	    const params = serializeFilters(next)
31	    const qs = params.toString()
32	    const path = route.path
33	    router.replace(qs ? `${path}?${qs}` : path)
34	  }
35	
36	  function hrefForFilters(partial: FilterUpdate, basePath = "/extensions") {
37	    const next: FilterUpdate = { ...filters.value, ...partial }
38	    if (!("page" in partial)) next.page = undefined
39	    const params = serializeFilters(next)
40	    const qs = params.toString()
41	    const path = localePath(basePath)
42	    return qs ? `${path}?${qs}` : path
43	  }
44	
45	  return { filters, update, hrefForFilters }
46	}
47	
```
