```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/pages/extensions/index.vue",
  "content": "<script setup lang=\"ts\">\nimport { ArrowUpRight, Search } from \"lucide-vue-next\"\n\ndefinePageMeta({ layout: \"browse\" })\n\nconst { t } = useI18n()\nconst route = useRoute()\nconst { filters, update } = useFilters()\nconst localePath = useLocalePath()\n\nconst { data, pending, refresh } = await useFetch(\"/api/internal/extensions\", {\n  query: computed(() => route.query),\n  default: () => ({ items: [], total: 0, filters: {} }),\n})\n\n// Facets don't depend on filters — fetch once and reuse across navigations.\nconst { data: facets } = await useFetch(\"/api/internal/facets\", {\n  default: () => ({ creators: [], publishers: [], tags: [] }),\n})\n\nwatch(() => route.fullPath, () => refresh())\n\nconst items = computed(() => data.value?.items ?? [])\nconst total = computed(() => data.value?.total ?? 0)\nconst query = computed(() => filters.value.q)\nconst filtersActive = computed(() => {\n  const f = filters.value\n  return Object.keys(f).some((k) => k !== \"page\" && f[k as keyof typeof f] !== undefined)\n})\nconst isMcpCategory = computed(() => route.query.category === \"mcp\")\n\nconst q = ref<string>(typeof route.query.q === \"string\" ? route.query.q : \"\")\nlet pushTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null\n\nfunction pushQuery() {\n  if (pushTimer) {\n    clearTimeout(pushTimer)\n    pushTimer = null\n  }\n  const trimmed = q.value.trim()\n  update({ q: trimmed || undefined })\n}\n\nfunction flushSearch() {\n  pushQuery()\n}\n\nwatch(q, () => {\n  if (pushTimer) clearTimeout(pushTimer)\n  pushTimer = setTimeout(pushQuery, 250)\n})\n\n// Keep the local input in sync when the URL changes from outside the input\n// (back button, chip dismissal, \"Clear all\").\nwatch(\n  () => route.query.q,\n  (next) => {\n    const incoming = typeof next === \"string\" ? next : \"\"\n    if (incoming !== q.value) q.value = incoming\n  },\n)\n\nonBeforeUnmount(() => {\n  if (pushTimer) clearTimeout(pushTimer)\n})\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <div class=\"px-6 py-8 max-w-7xl mx-auto\">\n    <div class=\"mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3\">\n      <form\n        role=\"search\"\n        class=\"relative flex-1 min-w-[240px]\"\n        @submit.prevent=\"flushSearch\"\n      >\n        <label class=\"sr-only\" for=\"extensions-search\">{{ t(\"nav.searchLabel\") }}</label>\n        <Search\n          :size=\"16\"\n          class=\"absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-(--color-ink-muted) pointer-events-none\"\n          aria-hidden=\"true\"\n        />\n        <input\n          id=\"extensions-search\"\n          v-model=\"q\"\n          type=\"search\"\n          :placeholder=\"t('search.placeholder')\"\n          class=\"w-full h-9 pl-9 pr-3 rounded-md border border-(--color-border) bg-(--color-bg) text-sm transition-colors focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-(--color-accent)\"\n        >\n      </form>\n      <NuxtLink\n        v-if=\"isMcpCategory\"\n        :to=\"localePath('/mcp-panorama')\"\n        class=\"inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[13px] font-medium text-(--color-accent) hover:gap-2 transition-[gap]\"\n      >\n        {{ t(\"extensions.mcpPanoramaLink\") }}\n        <ArrowUpRight :size=\"14\" aria-hidden=\"true\" />\n      </NuxtLink>\n      <SortSelect />\n    </div>\n\n    <FilterBar\n      :creators=\"facets.creators\"\n      :publishers=\"facets.publishers\"\n      :tags=\"facets.tags\"\n    />\n\n    <ResultsSummary\n      :count=\"total\"\n      :creators=\"facets.creators\"\n      :publishers=\"facets.publishers\"\n      :tags=\"facets.tags\"\n    />\n\n    <ExtGridSkeleton v-if=\"pending && items.length === 0\" />\n    <ExtGrid\n      v-else\n      :items=\"items\"\n      :query=\"query\"\n      :clear-filters-href=\"filtersActive ? localePath('/extensions') : undefined\"\n    />\n  </div>\n</template>\n"
}
```

```
3	// Browse-page golden path. The seed in scripts/seed.ts always lands at least
4	// one published extension, so an empty grid is a regression. These tests are
5	// SSR-only (request.get + HTML assertions) so they don't require a JS
6	// runtime and stay fast.
7	
8	test.describe("browse page", () => {
9	  test("/en/extensions returns 200 and renders the browse title", async ({ request }) => {
10	    const response = await request.get("/en/extensions")
11	    expect(response.status()).toBe(200)
12	    const html = await response.text()
13	    expect(html).toContain("Browse all")
14	  })

17	    const response = await request.get("/zh/extensions")
18	    expect(response.status()).toBe(200)
19	    const html = await response.text()
20	    // "浏览全部" or similar — covered by the locked Chinese-i18n decision.

25	    const response = await request.get("/en/extensions")
```

```json
  "command": "git commit -m \"$(cat <<'EOF'\nfeat(extensions): replace heading with search + sort toolbar\n\nPR #32 removed the global search input from the navbar with the\nrationale that search lives on /extensions — but the FilterBar\nnever had a search input. Land that promise: drop the static\n'Browse all' h1 and replace it with a slim toolbar: search input\non the left (flex-1, focus-ring, ⌘+Enter flushes), SortSelect on\nthe right. SortSelect moves out of ResultsSummary; the summary\nkeeps the count, chips, and 'Clear all'.\n\nSearch input is debounced ~250ms with useFilters().update — empty\ntrims clear the q param. A second watch syncs the input back when\nthe URL changes from outside (back button, chip dismissal).\n\nThe conditional MCP Panorama link (when category=mcp) moves into\nthe toolbar alongside Sort so it stays discoverable.\n\nTwo e2e tests asserted 'Browse all' and the removed Explore link\n— updated to assert the search placeholder and the new Skills\ninline nav link, plus stale 'Mode B' comment refreshed.\nEOF\n)\"",
```

```
1	# Extensions page polish: "All" category + search/sort toolbar
2	
3	## Context
4	
5	PR #32 (the navbar/sidebar refactor) is open on `refactor/layout-shell`. Two follow-up tweaks the user wants stacked onto the same branch:
6	
7	1. **Categories has no "All" row.** Today the sidebar clears the funcCat filter by clicking the active funcCat row a second time — the toggle is non-obvious. There's no dedicated affordance for "show all categories" from the sidebar.
8	2. **The `<h1>Browse all</h1>` heading on `/extensions` is dead weight.** It sits above the FilterBar adding visual mass without giving the user anything to do. Search was removed from the navbar in PR #32 with the rationale that search lives on `/extensions` via the FilterBar — but the FilterBar doesn't actually have a search input today. Replacing the heading with a slim **search-input + sort toolbar** lands that promise and reclaims vertical space.
9	
10	Decision #3 (single-row pill rail) is **not changing**. Tags / Scope / Dept / Creator / Publisher stay in the rail. The user opted for the conservative split.
11	
12	## Design decisions (confirmed)
13	
14	- **Sidebar:** add a single `All` row above the funcCat list. Highlighted when `funcCat`, `subCat`, `l2` are all unset. Clicking it clears those three params.
15	- **Top of `/extensions`:** drop the `<h1>` heading. Replace with a slim toolbar — search input on the left (full-flex), `SortSelect` on the right. The existing FilterBar (Scope / Dept / Creator / Publisher / TagDrawer) sits below the toolbar, unchanged.
```

> AGENT

Here's my honest take before I touch the plan:

**My recommendation: keep TagDrawer in the rail.** Reasons:

1. **Different mental model.** Categories is a *fixed taxonomy* (3 × 3 × 3). Tags is a *dynamic facet* — 30+ items, counts that change with other filters, multi-select with AND/OR mode. Sidebar nav reads as "click to drill in"; tag selection reads as "tick what applies." Mixing them creates ambiguity in one column.

2. **The TagDrawer is non-trivial UI.** Multi-select chips with counts + any/all toggle + show-more (currently 14-tag collapse). Cramming that into a 240 px sidebar column means either redesigning it or letting it eat ~300 px of vertical real estate.

3. **Decision-churn cost.** Locked decision #3 was already re-decided in May 2026 (multi-row drawer → single-row rail). Re-litigating eight months later, twice, undermines the "locked" part. If we change it, we should be paying for a real problem, not aesthetics.

4. **But there's a real underlying concern.** The TagDrawer is hidden behind a toggle with just a count badge — easy to miss. If your actual worry is **tag discoverability**, the better fix is to surface a small "Popular tags" links section in the sidebar *as a complement*, leaving the full multi-select machinery in the rail. That keeps both jobs honest.

Let me ask which problem you're trying to solve so I plan the right thing.

```
## Approved Plan:
# Decision recorded: keep TagDrawer in the pill rail (no code change)

## Context

After PR #32's navbar/sidebar refactor and the follow-up `feat(sidebar): add "All" row` + `feat(extensions): replace heading with search + sort toolbar` commits (both local on `refactor/layout-shell`), the user revisited the earlier question of whether to move tags from the FilterBar's TagDrawer into the sidebar.

User-stated motivation: **discoverability** — concern that the collapsed TagDrawer with its small count badge is too hidden for users to realize tag filtering exists.

User-chosen direction: **status quo — don't move anything**.

## Decision

**Tags stay in the FilterBar (`app/components/filters/TagDrawer.vue`). No code change.** Locked decision #3 (single-row quiet pill rail) is intact. CLAUDE.md and `docs/plan.md` are unchanged.

## Why this is the right call

1. **Different mental model.** Categories is a *fixed taxonomy* (3 funcCat × 3 l1 × 3 l2 from `shared/taxonomy.ts`). Tags is a *dynamic facet* — 30+ items, live counts, multi-select with AND/OR mode. Sidebar nav reads as "click to drill in"; tag selection reads as "tick what applies." Mixing both in one 240 px column muddies the affordance.
2. **TagDrawer is non-trivial UI.** Multi-select chips with counts + any/all toggle + 14-tag show-more collapse. Cramming this into the sidebar either redesigns it (cost) or eats ~300 px of vertical real estate (clutter).
3. **Decision churn is expensive.** Decision #3 was re-decided on 2026-05-21 (multi-row drawer → single-row rail). Re-litigating eight months later, twice in the same branch, would undermine the "locked" claim. If we revisit, it should be for a measured problem, not a hunch.

## If the discoverability concern returns later

Don't move the full TagDrawer — instead, **complement it** with a "Popular tags" links section in the sidebar:

- 8–12 top tags as compact single-click toggles (reuses the existing `update({ tags: [...] })` flow from `useFilters`).
- Pure discoverability surface — the rail-side TagDrawer keeps the multi-select + AND/OR machinery.
- Sidebar file: `app/components/layout/Sidebar.vue`, new section below the funcCat hierarchy.
- Data: same `/api/internal/facets` response that already feeds `FilterBar` — pass `facets.tags` down from `/app/pages/extensions/index.vue` via a sidebar prop or inject.
- Trigger this only if/when discoverability shows up as an actual user-reported problem; do not pre-emptively ship it.

## Verification

Nothing to verify in code — there are no changes.

This file exists as a written record of the decision so the next time someone asks "should tags go in the sidebar?", the prior reasoning is one read away.

```

Perfect! Now I have all the information. Let me compile the comprehensive report.

## Summary

### 1. **scripts/seed.ts** — Full Catalog Reset + Demo Seed
**Destructive**: YES — `TRUNCATE TABLE ${organizations}, ${tags} RESTART IDENTITY CASCADE` (line 121–122).

This is a **demo/development seed** that resets the entire catalog to a clean state. It:
- Truncates organizations and tags (with CASCADE to drop related data)
- Inserts hardcoded demo users (6 creators: Amy, Ben, Cory, Dao, Eli, Fei)
- Inserts departments and tags from data files
- Seeds **16 extensions** from `EXTENSIONS` array (line 197)
- Creates editorial collections from `COLLECTIONS` (line 234–266)
- Delegates to `seedMcpLandscape()` for the MCP panorama landscape

**Extension Counts**:
- **6 skills** (Web Search Pro, Code Interpreter, Image Vision, Arch Reviewer, Data Analyst, Test Generator)
- **4 mcp** (GitHub, Postgres, Slack, K8s)
- **3 slash** (/summarize, /translate, /explain)
- **3 plugins** (Notion Sync, Calendar Agent, MQTT Bridge)

**Code snippet** (lines 167–197):
```typescript
const extRows = EXTENSIONS.map((e, i) => {
  const creator = CREATORS[i % CREATORS.length]!
  const ownerOrgId = authorOrgIdMap.get(e.author)
  return {
    id: `ext-${e.id}`,
    slug: slugify(e.name),
    category: e.category,
    // ... further fields
    readmeMd: generateReadme(e),
  }
})
await db.insert(extensions).values(extRows)
```

---

### 2. **scripts/seed-mcp-landscape.ts** — Idempotent MCP Panorama
**Idempotent**: YES — Uses `onConflictDoUpdate` (upsert) for all rows. Safe for every deploy.

**Scope**: Seeds the static MCP landscape:
- **Taxonomy**: sectors, domains, PDTs (product/domain/technology triplets)
- **Extension stubs**: One per released MCP (marketplace listings), with id prefix `mcp-*`
- **Landscape tools & MCPs**: Per-MCP rows linked to tools, with dev/released status, deps, tags

**Why Safe for vercel-build**: 
- No destructive deletes except for stale MCPs (line 259): prunes rows no longer in the canonical `MCP_TOOLS` inventory
- Extensions with real publishers (id prefix NOT `mcp-*`) are untouched
- Organizations and users are untouched
- All upserts use `onConflictDoNothing` or `onConflictDoUpdate`, never truncate

**Code snippet** (lines 45–57):
```typescript
await db
  .insert(organizations)
  .values({
    id: SYSTEM_ORG_ID,
    slug: SYSTEM_ORG_ID,
    name: "Default Organization",
    nameZh: "默认组织",
  })
  .onConflictDoNothing({ target: organizations.id })
```

---

### 3. **scripts/seed-editorial-collections.ts** — Graceful Collection Curation
**Idempotent**: YES — Uses `onConflictDoNothing` for collection items (line 150). Creates collections only when slug is free (line 114–138).

**Idempotency Strategy**:
- Resolves owner emails and extension slugs in two batched queries upfront (lines 64–78)
- Skips collections whose owner email is missing (non-blocking)
- Skips individual items with missing extension slugs, but still creates the collection
- `onConflictDoNothing` on `(collectionId, extensionId)` composite PK prevents re-run errors
- Existing collection metadata is untouched (no overwrites)

**Code snippet** (lines 147–152):
```typescript
const inserted = await db
  .insert(collectionItems)
  .values(resolvedItems)
  .onConflictDoNothing()
  .returning({ extensionId: collectionItems.extensionId })
```

---

### 4. **Extension Definitions Location**
**Hardcoded in data files** (not separate schema):
- `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/shared/data/extensions.ts` — 16 mock extensions
- `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/shared/data/mcp-landscape.ts` — MCP tools, MCPs, sectors, domains, PDTs
- `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/shared/data/collections.ts` — Editorial collections (curated listings)
- `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/shared/data/departments.ts` — Department tree (3 levels)

All are TS arrays exported at module load; the seeds materialize them into the database.

---

### 5. **Extension Schema** — `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/shared/db/schema/extension.ts`
**Primary Table: `extensions`**

| Column | Type | Notes |
|--------|------|-------|
| `id` | text PK | e.g., `ext-1`, `mcp-{slug}` |
| `slug` | text unique | URL slug, auto-derived from name |
| `category` | enum | `skills`, `mcp`, `slash`, `plugins` |
| `scope` | enum | `personal`, `org`, `enterprise` |
| `badge` | enum | `official`, `popular`, `new` (nullable) |
| `funcCat` | enum | `workTask`, `business`, `tools` (nullable) |
| `subCat` | text | e.g., `"docs"`, `"softDev"` (nullable) |
| `publisherUserId` | text FK | Refs `users.id`, sets null on delete |
| `ownerOrgId` | text FK | Refs `organizations.id`, cascades on delete |
| `deptId` | text FK | Refs `departments.id`, sets null on delete |
| `visibility` | enum | `draft`, `published`, `archived` |
| `featured` | boolean | Editorial homepage pick (default false) |
| `name`, `nameZh` | text | Display names (i18n) |
| `tagline`, `taglineZh` | text | Short blurb (nullable) |
| `description`, `descriptionZh` | text | Long description (nullable) |
| `readmeMd` | text | Markdown for detail page (nullable) |
| `iconEmoji`, `iconColor` | text | Branding (nullable) |
| `downloadsCount`, `starsAvg`, `ratingsCount` | int/numeric | Denormalized metrics |
| `publishedAt` | timestamp tz | Publication date (nullable) |
| `createdAt`, `updatedAt` | timestamp tz | Auto-managed |

Related tables:
- `extensionTags` (many-to-many with `tags`, composite PK on extensionId+tagId)
- `extensionVersions` (one extension, multiple versioned releases)
- `files` (uploaded zip bundles linked to versions)

---

### 6. **Departments, Users, Tags**
All are seeded by `scripts/seed.ts`:

- **Departments**: From `/shared/data/departments.ts` — nested tree with 3 levels (e.g., `eng.cloud.infra`)
- **Creator Users**: Hardcoded in `seed.ts` lines 26–33 (6 demo users with `@agentcenter.dev` emails)
- **Tags**: From `/shared/tags.ts` (line 159–165 in seed.ts) — 36 tags with EN/ZH labels (search, real-time, stable, python, etc.)

Extensions reference departments (via `deptId`), users (via `publisherUserId`), and tags (via `extensionTags` junction table). All lookups are by ID or slug at seed time.

**Location:** `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/shared/types.ts` (line 5) and `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/shared/db/schema/extension.ts` (lines 28-33)

```
type ExtensionCategory = "skills" | "mcp" | "slash" | "plugins"
```

Enum also defined in DB schema via `pgEnum("extension_category", ["skills", "mcp", "slash", "plugins"])`.

### 2. Skill-Specific Data Shape
Skills use the **generic Extension interface** — no special skill-only fields. However, note that the DB schema (`extension.ts`) includes optional fields useful for skills:

**Core fields (all extensions):**
- `id`, `slug`, `category`, `scope`, `funcCat`, `subCat`, `l2`
- `name`, `nameZh`, `tagline`, `taglineZh`, `description`, `descriptionZh`
- `badge`, `featured`, `visibility`
- `deptId`, `ownerOrgId`, `publisherUserId`

**Rich metadata (optional):**
- `iconEmoji`, `iconColor`
- `homepageUrl`, `repoUrl`, `licenseSpdx`
- `compatibilityJson`, `permissions`, `readmeMd`
- `downloadsCount`, `starsAvg`, `ratingsCount`, `publishedAt`

The **mock Extension interface** in `/shared/types.ts` is simpler (used for seed data):
```
id, dept, tags, name, nameZh, author, category, badge, 
downloads, stars, color, icon, funcCat, subCat, scope, desc, descZh
```

```json
  "content": "# Seed catalog: 50+ skills + filler slash/plugins, wired into vercel-build\n\n## Context\n\nThe dev seed (`scripts/seed.ts`) creates **only 6 skills + 4 MCPs + 3 slash + 3 plugins** — and it's destructive (`TRUNCATE … RESTART IDENTITY CASCADE`), so it's deliberately **not** in `vercel-build`. The user sees ~2 skills on prod and wants 50+, with the seeder safe to run on every deploy.\n\n## Decision\n\nAdd a new idempotent catalog seeder that mirrors the pattern proven by `seed-mcp-landscape.ts` and `seed-editorial-collections.ts`. Don't touch `seed.ts` — it stays the destructive demo seed for dev. The new seeder lives next to its siblings in `vercel-build`.\n\nUser-confirmed scope:\n- **50+ skills** spread across the 3 × 3 funcCat/L1 taxonomy (target: ~54, six per L1 group).\n- **~5–7 slash commands** and **~5–7 plugins** so the four primary nav links each show a healthy grid.\n- Stack on the existing `refactor/layout-shell` branch (commits flow into PR #32 when pushed).\n\n## Architecture: how this stays safe in vercel-build\n\nMirrors `scripts/seed-mcp-landscape.ts` line-for-line in idempotency strategy:\n\n1. **Distinct id prefix.** Every catalog row uses `cat-{slug}` (parallel to `mcp-{slug}`). The seeder only touches rows whose id matches `LIKE 'cat-%'`. Real user-published extensions (any other prefix) are never read or written.\n2. **Upserts, not truncates.** `onConflictDoUpdate` on the extension `id` for content updates; `onConflictDoNothing` for join-table rows.\n3. **Self-sufficient prerequisites.** The seeder upserts everything it needs:\n   - `SYSTEM_ORG_ID = \"default\"` (same constant as mcp-landscape; safe to upsert twice).\n   - All tags referenced by catalog rows — pulled from `shared/tags.ts` `TAG_LABELS`, upserted via `onConflictDoNothing` on `id`.\n   - `publisherUserId: null` everywhere — schema allows it (no `notNull()` on that column per `shared/db/schema/extension.ts:85`). No dependency on demo users existing.\n   - `deptId: null` — allowed; departments are still optional. (Demo departments come from `seed.ts` and only matter in dev.)\n4. **Stale-row pruning.** After upserts, delete any `id LIKE 'cat-%'` not in the desired set (same pattern as `seed-mcp-landscape.ts:272–279`). Lets us remove entries from the catalog file and have prod converge on the next deploy.\n5. **Halt with a clear message** on any unexpected schema mismatch instead of partial writes.\n\n## Files\n\n| File | Change |\n|---|---|\n| `shared/data/catalog.ts` | **new** — array of ~66 entries (54 skills + 6 slash + 6 plugins). Each entry: slug, name+nameZh, tagline+taglineZh, description+descriptionZh, category, funcCat/subCat/l2, iconEmoji+iconColor, tags[], optional badge, downloads, starsAvg. Type matches a new local `CatalogEntry` interface; existing `shared/data/extensions.ts` left alone. |\n| `scripts/seed-catalog.ts` | **new** — idempotent seeder. Steps: upsert SYSTEM_ORG → upsert tags from `shared/tags.ts` (existing helper `TAG_LABELS`) → upsert extensions from `CATALOG` with `id = \"cat-${slug}\"`, `ownerOrgId = SYSTEM_ORG_ID`, `publisherUserId = null`, `visibility = \"published\"`, `publishedAt = now()` → replace each extension's tag rows (delete-then-insert on `extensionTags` filtered by extensionId) → prune stale `cat-%` rows not in CATALOG. ~150 lines. |\n| `package.json` | Append `&& bun scripts/seed-catalog.ts` to the existing `vercel-build` chain (after `seed-editorial-collections`, before `nuxt build`). Add `db:seed:catalog` dev script for ad-hoc runs. |\n| (no schema changes) | The extension and extension_tags schemas already support everything we need. |\n\n## Skill distribution (54 entries, 6 per L1 group)\n\nEach L1 gets 6 hand-picked skills with realistic taglines + zh translations + tag mix. Sketch:\n\n- **workTask · systemDesign** (reqAnalysis/funcDesign/archDesign): user-story-writer, requirements-elicitor, c4-diagrammer, system-design-reviewer, api-spec-helper, schema-modeler\n- **workTask · softDev** (frontend/backend/devops): tailwind-helper, react-hook-genie, vue-composable-builder, sql-tuner, fastapi-scaffold, dockerfile-linter\n- **workTask · testing** (unitTest/intTest/perfTest): vitest-case-writer, jest-mock-builder, playwright-recorder, contract-test-helper, k6-load-script, mutation-test-runner\n- **business · network** (http/grpc/mqtt): postman-helper, grpc-proto-gen, mqtt-debugger, har-analyzer, openapi-linter, websocket-tester\n- **business · embedded** (rtos/firmware/drivers): freertos-helper, esp-idf-scaffold, kernel-module-template, i2c-debugger, hex-inspector, flash-tool-helper\n- **business · cloud** (aws/azure/k8s): aws-iam-policy-helper, k8s-manifest-gen, terraform-validator, helm-chart-creator, gcp-iam-helper, azure-bicep-gen\n- **tools · docs** (markdown/pdf/wiki): markdown-linter, mermaid-diagrammer, confluence-formatter, pdf-toc-builder, readme-generator, docusaurus-helper\n- **tools · data** (csv/sql/viz): csv-to-sql, query-explainer, chart-suggester, data-pivot-helper, regex-builder, json-schema-helper\n- **tools · vcs** (git/pr/cicd): git-conflict-helper, pr-description-writer, conventional-commit-helper, gitignore-builder, semver-bumper, github-actions-helper\n\n**Slash (6):** /summarize-pr, /explain-error, /test-this, /draft-doc, /refactor-suggest, /commit-message\n\n**Plugins (6):** notion-sync, calendar-agent, mqtt-bridge (already seeded by seed.ts but with `ext-` ids — catalog adds the `cat-` variant for prod parity), jira-link, sentry-bridge, linear-bridge\n\nFor each entry: a short tagline (~70 chars) + a 1–2 sentence description, both EN and ZH. Icon emoji + accent color from a small curated palette (no `--color-accent` reuse — per CLAUDE.md §11). 4–6 tags drawn from `shared/tags.ts`'s 36-item vocab. Plausible `downloadsCount` (200–250k range, log-distributed) and `starsAvg` (4.3–4.9). One in ~10 carries a `badge` (`official` / `popular` / `new`) so the badge UI keeps rendering.\n\n## Commits, branch, push\n\nStack on `refactor/layout-shell` (already 2 commits ahead of origin). Two commits:\n\n1. **`feat(seed): add catalog data file with 54 skills + 12 fillers`** — `shared/data/catalog.ts` only. Compilable but unused.\n2. **`feat(seed): add idempotent catalog seeder; wire into vercel-build`** — `scripts/seed-catalog.ts`, `package.json` updates. Self-contained; running it locally inserts everything cleanly.\n\nPer `CLAUDE.md`: no `Co-Authored-By:` trailer, no Claude Code footer, no `--no-verify`. Run `bun run validate` after both commits. **Do not push** unless explicitly asked — PR #32 will pick these up when finally pushed.\n\n## Verification\n\n1. `bun scripts/seed-catalog.ts` locally against the dev DB. Expect: log line per upsert phase, final summary like `Catalog: 54 skills + 6 slash + 6 plugins upserted, 0 pruned`. Re-running immediately produces the same summary with no diffs (idempotent).\n2. Manually break idempotency check: delete one entry from `catalog.ts`, re-run → summary shows `pruned: 1` for that slug. Restore entry → next run upserts it back.\n3. `bun run validate` — lint + typecheck + 242 tests. Should stay green; no test asserts a specific extension count.\n4. `bun dev` → `/extensions?category=skills` shows the new catalog entries (with the page's 24-per-page default, scroll/paginate confirms 50+).\n5. `/extensions?funcCat=workTask` shows a spread of skills from each L1 under workTask. Same for `business` and `tools`.\n6. Tag drawer on `/extensions` shows non-zero counts for at least 20 of the 36 tags.\n7. Spot-check one detail page (`/extensions/<some-skill-slug>`): renders tagline, description, emoji, tags.\n\n## Out of scope\n\n- Adding/changing the MCP catalog — `seed-mcp-landscape.ts` already covers that.\n- Touching `scripts/seed.ts` (the destructive demo seed). It stays as-is for dev convenience.\n- New extension types / schema changes.\n- Per-extension version rows (`extensionVersions`) — leaving null so the detail page falls back to the listing-only rendering path; bundle uploads remain user-driven.\n- Real authorship via `publisherUserId` — null is correct for catalog-managed entries; if we later want a \"Catalog\" virtual publisher, that's a follow-up.\n- Localization beyond EN/ZH (other locales aren't supported).\n"
}
```

```
## Approved Plan:
# Seed catalog: 50+ skills + filler slash/plugins, wired into vercel-build

## Context

The dev seed (`scripts/seed.ts`) creates **only 6 skills + 4 MCPs + 3 slash + 3 plugins** — and it's destructive (`TRUNCATE … RESTART IDENTITY CASCADE`), so it's deliberately **not** in `vercel-build`. Production therefore has whatever `seed-mcp-landscape.ts` writes (MCP-prefixed entries) plus nothing for the other three categories. The user sees ~2 skills on prod and wants 50+, with the seeder safe to run on every deploy.

## Decision

Add a new idempotent catalog seeder that mirrors the pattern proven by `seed-mcp-landscape.ts` and `seed-editorial-collections.ts`. Don't touch `seed.ts` — it stays the destructive demo seed for dev. The new seeder lives next to its siblings in `vercel-build`.

User-confirmed scope:
- **50+ skills** spread across the 3 × 3 funcCat/L1 taxonomy (target: ~54, six per L1 group).
- **~5–7 slash commands** and **~5–7 plugins** so the four primary nav links each show a healthy grid.
- Stack on the existing `refactor/layout-shell` branch (commits flow into PR #32 when pushed).

## Architecture: how this stays safe in vercel-build

Mirrors `scripts/seed-mcp-landscape.ts` line-for-line in idempotency strategy:

1. **Distinct id prefix.** Every catalog row uses `cat-{slug}` (parallel to `mcp-{slug}`). The seeder only touches rows whose id matches `LIKE 'cat-%'`. Real user-published extensions (any other prefix) are never read or written.
2. **Upserts, not truncates.** `onConflictDoUpdate` on the extension `id` for content updates; `onConflictDoNothing` for join-table rows.
3. **Self-sufficient prerequisites.** The seeder upserts everything it needs:
   - `SYSTEM_ORG_ID = "default"` (same constant as mcp-landscape; safe to upsert twice).
   - All tags referenced by catalog rows — pulled from `shared/tags.ts` `TAG_LABELS`, upserted via `onConflictDoNothing` on `id`.
   - `publisherUserId: null` everywhere — schema allows it (no `notNull()` on that column per `shared/db/schema/extension.ts:85`). No dependency on demo users existing.
   - `deptId: null` — allowed; departments are still optional. (Demo departments come from `seed.ts` and only matter in dev.)
4. **Stale-row pruning.** After upserts, delete any `id LIKE 'cat-%'` not in the desired set (same pattern as `seed-mcp-landscape.ts:272–279`). Lets us remove entries from the catalog file and have prod converge on the next deploy.
5. **Halt with a clear message** on any unexpected schema mismatch instead of partial writes.

## Files

| File | Change |
|---|---|
| `shared/data/catalog.ts` | **new** — array of ~66 entries (54 skills + 6 slash + 6 plugins). Each entry: slug, name+nameZh, tagline+taglineZh, description+descriptionZh, category, funcCat/subCat/l2, iconEmoji+iconColor, tags[], optional badge, downloads, starsAvg. Type matches a new local `CatalogEntry` interface; existing `shared/data/extensions.ts` left alone. |
| `scripts/seed-catalog.ts` | **new** — idempotent seeder. Steps: upsert SYSTEM_ORG → upsert tags from `shared/tags.ts` (existing helper `TAG_LABELS`) → upsert extensions from `CATALOG` with `id = "cat-${slug}"`, `ownerOrgId = SYSTEM_ORG_ID`, `publisherUserId = null`, `visibility = "published"`, `publishedAt = now()` → replace each extension's tag rows (delete-then-insert on `extensionTags` filtered by extensionId) → prune stale `cat-%` rows not in CATALOG. ~150 lines. |
| `package.json` | Append `&& bun scripts/seed-catalog.ts` to the existing `vercel-build` chain (after `seed-editorial-collections`, before `nuxt build`). Add `db:seed:catalog` dev script for ad-hoc runs. |
| (no schema changes) | The extension and extension_tags schemas already support everything we need. |

## Skill distribution (54 entries, 6 per L1 group)

Each L1 gets 6 hand-picked skills with realistic taglines + zh translations + tag mix. Sketch:

- **workTask · systemDesign** (reqAnalysis/funcDesign/archDesign): user-story-writer, requirements-elicitor, c4-diagrammer, system-design-reviewer, api-spec-helper, schema-modeler
- **workTask · softDev** (frontend/backend/devops): tailwind-helper, react-hook-genie, vue-composable-builder, sql-tuner, fastapi-scaffold, dockerfile-linter
- **workTask · testing** (unitTest/intTest/perfTest): vitest-case-writer, jest-mock-builder, playwright-recorder, contract-test-helper, k6-load-script, mutation-test-runner
- **business · network** (http/grpc/mqtt): postman-helper, grpc-proto-gen, mqtt-debugger, har-analyzer, openapi-linter, websocket-tester
- **business · embedded** (rtos/firmware/drivers): freertos-helper, esp-idf-scaffold, kernel-module-template, i2c-debugger, hex-inspector, flash-tool-helper
- **business · cloud** (aws/azure/k8s): aws-iam-policy-helper, k8s-manifest-gen, terraform-validator, helm-chart-creator, gcp-iam-helper, azure-bicep-gen
- **tools · docs** (markdown/pdf/wiki): markdown-linter, mermaid-diagrammer, confluence-formatter, pdf-toc-builder, readme-generator, docusaurus-helper
- **tools · data** (csv/sql/viz): csv-to-sql, query-explainer, chart-suggester, data-pivot-helper, regex-builder, json-schema-helper
- **tools · vcs** (git/pr/cicd): git-conflict-helper, pr-description-writer, conventional-commit-helper, gitignore-builder, semver-bumper, github-actions-helper

**Slash (6):** /summarize-pr, /explain-error, /test-this, /draft-doc, /refactor-suggest, /commit-message

**Plugins (6):** notion-sync, calendar-agent, mqtt-bridge (already seeded by seed.ts but with `ext-` ids — catalog adds the `cat-` variant for prod parity), jira-link, sentry-bridge, linear-bridge

For each entry: a short tagline (~70 chars) + a 1–2 sentence description, both EN and ZH. Icon emoji + accent color from a small curated palette (no `--color-accent` reuse — per CLAUDE.md §11). 4–6 tags drawn from `shared/tags.ts`'s 36-item vocab. Plausible `downloadsCount` (200–250k range, log-distributed) and `starsAvg` (4.3–4.9). One in ~10 carries a `badge` (`official` / `popular` / `new`) so the badge UI keeps rendering.

## Commits, branch, push

Stack on `refactor/layout-shell` (already 2 commits ahead of origin). Two commits:

1. **`feat(seed): add catalog data file with 54 skills + 12 fillers`** — `shared/data/catalog.ts` only. Compilable but unused.
2. **`feat(seed): add idempotent catalog seeder; wire into vercel-build`** — `scripts/seed-catalog.ts`, `package.json` updates. Self-contained; running it locally inserts everything cleanly.

Per `CLAUDE.md`: no `Co-Authored-By:` trailer, no Claude Code footer, no `--no-verify`. Run `bun run validate` after both commits. **Do not push** unless explicitly asked — PR #32 will pick these up when finally pushed.

## Verification

1. `bun scripts/seed-catalog.ts` locally against the dev DB. Expect: log line per upsert phase, final summary like `Catalog: 54 skills + 6 slash + 6 plugins upserted, 0 pruned`. Re-running immediately produces the same summary with no diffs (idempotent).
2. Manually break idempotency check: delete one entry from `catalog.ts`, re-run → summary shows `pruned: 1` for that slug. Restore entry → next run upserts it back.
3. `bun run validate` — lint + typecheck + 242 tests. Should stay green; no test asserts a specific extension count.
4. `bun dev` → `/extensions?category=skills` shows the new catalog entries (with the page's 24-per-page default, scroll/paginate confirms 50+).
5. `/extensions?funcCat=workTask` shows a spread of skills from each L1 under workTask. Same for `business` and `tools`.
6. Tag drawer on `/extensions` shows non-zero counts for at least 20 of the 36 tags.
7. Spot-check one detail page (`/extensions/<some-skill-slug>`): renders tagline, description, emoji, tags.

## Out of scope

- Adding/changing the MCP catalog — `seed-mcp-landscape.ts` already covers that.
- Touching `scripts/seed.ts` (the destructive demo seed). It stays as-is for dev convenience.
- New extension types / schema changes.
- Per-extension version rows (`extensionVersions`) — leaving null so the detail page falls back to the listing-only rendering path; bundle uploads remain user-driven.
- Real authorship via `publisherUserId` — null is correct for catalog-managed entries; if we later want a "Catalog" virtual publisher, that's a follow-up.
- Localization beyond EN/ZH (other locales aren't supported).

```

```
1	import { sql } from "drizzle-orm";
2	import {
3	  bigint,
4	  boolean,
5	  customType,
6	  index,
7	  integer,
8	  jsonb,
9	  numeric,
10	  pgEnum,
11	  pgTable,
12	  primaryKey,
13	  text,
14	  timestamp,
15	  unique,
16	} from "drizzle-orm/pg-core";
17	
18	import { users } from "./auth";
19	import { departments, organizations } from "./org";
20	
21	// tsvector is a Postgres-native type; Drizzle doesn't ship a built-in helper.
22	// We declare it as a custom type so the ORM can reference it in WHERE clauses
23	// via sql``. It is GENERATED ALWAYS (read-only); never written by app code.
24	const tsvector = customType<{ data: string }>({
25	  dataType() { return "tsvector"; },
26	});
27	
28	export const extensionCategoryEnum = pgEnum("extension_category", [
29	  "skills",
30	  "mcp",
31	  "slash",
32	  "plugins",
33	]);
34	
35	export const extensionScopeEnum = pgEnum("extension_scope", [
36	  "personal",
37	  "org",
38	  "enterprise",
39	]);
40	
41	export const extensionBadgeEnum = pgEnum("extension_badge", [
42	  "official",
43	  "popular",
44	  "new",
45	]);
46	
47	export const funcCatEnum = pgEnum("func_cat", [
48	  "workTask",
49	  "business",
50	  "tools",
51	]);
52	
53	export const visibilityEnum = pgEnum("extension_visibility", [
54	  "draft",
55	  "published",
56	  "archived",
57	]);
58	
59	export const versionStatusEnum = pgEnum("version_status", [
60	  "pending",
61	  "scanning",
62	  "ready",
63	  "rejected",
64	]);
65	
66	export const fileScanStatusEnum = pgEnum("file_scan_status", [
67	  "pending",
68	  "clean",
69	  "flagged",
70	]);
71	
72	export const extensions = pgTable(
73	  "extensions",
74	  {
75	    id: text().primaryKey(),
76	    slug: text().notNull().unique(),
77	    category: extensionCategoryEnum().notNull(),
78	    badge: extensionBadgeEnum(),
79	    scope: extensionScopeEnum().notNull(),
80	    // funcCat/subCat are nullable — the redesigned publish wizard does not
81	    // collect them; admin curation can backfill or system defaults apply.
82	    funcCat: funcCatEnum(),
83	    subCat: text(),
84	    l2: text(),
85	    publisherUserId: text().references(() => users.id, {
86	      onDelete: "set null",
87	    }),
88	    ownerOrgId: text()
89	      .notNull()
90	      .references(() => organizations.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
91	    deptId: text().references(() => departments.id, { onDelete: "set null" }),
92	    iconEmoji: text(),
93	    iconColor: text(),
94	    visibility: visibilityEnum().notNull().default("draft"),
95	    // Editorial flag — hand-curated. Surfaces a single extension on the home
96	    // hero. Coexists with `badge`: badge is a static editorial mark on the
97	    // card; `featured` is a transient "pick of the week" pointer.
98	    featured: boolean().notNull().default(false),
99	    // i18n columns
100	    name: text().notNull(),
101	    nameZh: text(),
102	    tagline: text(),
103	    taglineZh: text(),
104	    description: text(),
105	    descriptionZh: text(),
106	    // Long-form markdown body for the detail page. Not duplicated to ZH yet —
107	    // most extensions will have an English README, with translations layered
108	    // on later if needed.
109	    readmeMd: text(),
110	    // metadata for detail page sidebar
111	    homepageUrl: text(),
112	    repoUrl: text(),
113	    licenseSpdx: text(),
114	    compatibilityJson: jsonb(),
115	    // Permissions captured during publish (network/files/runtime/data, etc.).
116	    // Surfaced on the detail page so users see what the extension wants.
117	    permissions: jsonb().notNull().default({}),
118	    // denormalized counters (updated by jobs)
119	    downloadsCount: integer().notNull().default(0),
120	    starsAvg: numeric({ precision: 2, scale: 1 }).notNull().default("0.0"),
121	    ratingsCount: integer().notNull().default(0),
122	    publishedAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }),
123	    createdAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),
124	    updatedAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),
125	    searchVector: tsvector("search_vector"),
126	  },
127	  (t) => [
128	    index("idx_ext_category").on(t.category),
129	    index("idx_ext_scope").on(t.scope),
130	    index("idx_ext_func_sub_l2").on(t.funcCat, t.subCat, t.l2),
131	    index("idx_ext_dept_path").using(
132	      "btree",
133	      sql`${t.deptId} text_pattern_ops`,
134	    ),
135	    index("idx_ext_visibility").on(t.visibility),
136	    index("idx_ext_downloads").on(sql`${t.downloadsCount} DESC`),
137	    index("idx_ext_stars").on(sql`${t.starsAvg} DESC`),
138	    index("idx_ext_featured_published").on(
139	      t.featured,
140	      sql`${t.publishedAt} DESC`,
141	    ),
142	  ],
143	);
144	
145	export const extensionVersions = pgTable(
146	  "extension_versions",
147	  {
148	    id: text().primaryKey(),
149	    extensionId: text()
150	      .notNull()
151	      .references(() => extensions.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
152	    version: text().notNull(), // semver
153	    changelog: text(),
154	    changelogZh: text(),
155	    manifestJson: jsonb(),
156	    // Logical reference to files.id; no FK constraint to avoid a circular FK
157	    // with files.extensionVersionId. App code keeps these consistent.
158	    bundleFileId: text(),
159	    status: versionStatusEnum().notNull().default("pending"),
160	    // Source method chosen during publish: zip (only one wired today), git, cli.
161	    // Stored as text rather than enum so adding methods doesn't require a migration.
162	    sourceMethod: text().notNull().default("zip"),
163	    sourceMeta: jsonb().notNull().default({}),
164	    publishedAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }),
165	    createdAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),
166	  },
167	  (t) => [unique("ext_version_unique").on(t.extensionId, t.version)],
168	);
169	
170	export const files = pgTable("files", {
171	  id: text().primaryKey(),
172	  extensionVersionId: text().references(() => extensionVersions.id, {
173	    onDelete: "set null",
174	  }),
175	  r2Key: text().notNull(),
176	  size: bigint({ mode: "bigint" }).notNull(),
177	  checksumSha256: text().notNull(),
178	  mimeType: text(),
179	  scanStatus: fileScanStatusEnum().notNull().default("pending"),
180	  scanReport: jsonb(),
181	  createdAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),
182	});
183	
184	export const tags = pgTable("tags", {
185	  id: text().primaryKey(), // tag key, e.g. "real-time"
186	  labelEn: text().notNull(),
187	  labelZh: text().notNull(),
188	});
189	
190	export const extensionTags = pgTable(
191	  "extension_tags",
192	  {
193	    extensionId: text()
194	      .notNull()
195	      .references(() => extensions.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
196	    tagId: text()
197	      .notNull()
198	      .references(() => tags.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
199	  },
200	  (t) => [
201	    primaryKey({ columns: [t.extensionId, t.tagId] }),
202	    index("idx_extension_tags_tag_ext").on(t.tagId, t.extensionId),
203	  ],
204	);
205
```

```
1	// Idempotent MCP-landscape seed.
2	//
3	// Safe to run on every deploy. Unlike scripts/seed.ts (which TRUNCATEs to
4	// produce a clean demo state), this script only upserts the static MCP
5	// landscape — taxonomy + tool rows + per-MCP rows + marketplace extension
6	// stubs the panorama links to. Re-running has no destructive effect on user
7	// data, other extensions, departments, tags, etc.
8	//
9	// Wired into the Vercel build via `vercel-build` so the panorama page
10	// always has data on a freshly deployed environment.
11	//
12	// Also imported by scripts/seed.ts so the demo seed and the standalone
13	// seed share one source of truth.
14	
15	import { and, notInArray, sql } from "drizzle-orm"
16	import { drizzle, type PostgresJsDatabase } from "drizzle-orm/postgres-js"
17	import postgres from "postgres"
18	
19	import {
20	  INDUSTRY_SECTORS,
21	  MCP_TOOLS,
22	  PUBLIC_DOMAINS,
23	  ownerToParts,
24	} from "../shared/data/mcp-landscape"
25	import * as schema from "../shared/db/schema"
26	import {
27	  extensions,
28	  mcpDomains,
29	  mcpLandscapeMcps,
30	  mcpLandscapeTools,
31	  mcpPdts,
32	  mcpSectors,
33	  organizations,
34	} from "../shared/db/schema"
35	
36	type Db = PostgresJsDatabase<typeof schema>
37	
38	const SYSTEM_ORG_ID = "default"
39	
40	/**
41	 * Upserts the static MCP landscape: a single owner org for the marketplace
42	 * stubs, the sector/domain/PDT taxonomy, the per-MCP extension stubs, the
43	 * landscape tool rows, and the per-MCP rows. Re-runnable; never destructive.
44	 */
45	export async function seedMcpLandscape(db: Db): Promise<void> {
46	  // Owner org for the marketplace extension stubs. We upsert by id so the
47	  // demo seed's `default` org (when present) is preserved verbatim, and a
48	  // fresh deploy can stand the row up on its own.
49	  await db
50	    .insert(organizations)
51	    .values({
52	      id: SYSTEM_ORG_ID,
53	      slug: SYSTEM_ORG_ID,
54	      name: "Default Organization",
55	      nameZh: "默认组织",
56	    })
57	    .onConflictDoNothing({ target: organizations.id })
58	
59	  // Sectors.
60	  const sectorRows = INDUSTRY_SECTORS.map((s, i) => ({
61	    key: s.key,
62	    label: s.label,
63	    labelZh: s.labelZh,
64	    short: s.short,
65	    sortOrder: i,
66	  }))
67	  console.log(`seed-mcp: upserting ${sectorRows.length} sectors`)
68	  await db
69	    .insert(mcpSectors)
70	    .values(sectorRows)
71	    .onConflictDoUpdate({
72	      target: mcpSectors.key,
73	      set: {
74	        label: sql`excluded.label`,
75	        labelZh: sql`excluded.label_zh`,
76	        short: sql`excluded.short`,
77	        sortOrder: sql`excluded.sort_order`,
78	      },
79	    })
80	
81	  // Domains.
82	  const domainRows = PUBLIC_DOMAINS.map((d, i) => ({
83	    key: d.key,
84	    label: d.label,
85	    labelZh: d.labelZh,
86	    short: d.short,
87	    sortOrder: i,
88	  }))
89	  console.log(`seed-mcp: upserting ${domainRows.length} domains`)
90	  await db
91	    .insert(mcpDomains)
92	    .values(domainRows)
93	    .onConflictDoUpdate({
94	      target: mcpDomains.key,
95	      set: {
96	        label: sql`excluded.label`,
97	        labelZh: sql`excluded.label_zh`,
98	        short: sql`excluded.short`,
99	        sortOrder: sql`excluded.sort_order`,
100	      },
101	    })
102	
103	  // PDTs.
104	  const pdtRows = PUBLIC_DOMAINS.flatMap((d) =>
105	    d.pdts.map((p, i) => ({
106	      key: `${d.key}.${p.key}`,
107	      domainKey: d.key,
108	      label: p.label,
109	      labelZh: p.labelZh,
110	      sortOrder: i,
111	    })),
112	  )
113	  console.log(`seed-mcp: upserting ${pdtRows.length} PDTs`)
114	  await db
115	    .insert(mcpPdts)
116	    .values(pdtRows)
117	    .onConflictDoUpdate({
118	      target: mcpPdts.key,
119	      set: {
120	        domainKey: sql`excluded.domain_key`,
121	        label: sql`excluded.label`,
122	        labelZh: sql`excluded.label_zh`,
123	        sortOrder: sql`excluded.sort_order`,
124	      },
125	    })
126	
127	  // Marketplace MCP extension stubs — one per released MCP across all tools.
128	  // The extension's name/blurb mirror the MCP (not the parent tool) so the
129	  // marketplace listing reads coherently. For tools where the MCP doesn't
130	  // carry its own name, fall back to the tool's display name.
131	  const releasedMcpRows = MCP_TOOLS.flatMap((t) =>
132	    t.mcps
133	      .filter((m) => m.released)
134	      .map((m) => ({ tool: t, mcp: m })),
135	  )
136	  const mcpExtRows = releasedMcpRows.map(({ mcp: m }) => {
137	    // MCPs are identified by their slug — that's the canonical display label
138	    // across the marketplace listing, the panorama tile, and the detail panel.
139	    const name = m.name ?? m.slug
140	    const nameZh = m.nameZh ?? null
141	    return {
142	      id: `mcp-${m.slug}`,
143	      slug: m.slug,
144	      category: "mcp" as const,
145	      badge: null,
146	      scope: "enterprise" as const,
147	      funcCat: null,
148	      subCat: null,
149	      publisherUserId: null,
150	      ownerOrgId: SYSTEM_ORG_ID,
151	      deptId: null,
152	      iconEmoji: null,
153	      iconColor: null,
154	      visibility: "published" as const,
155	      name,
156	      nameZh,
157	      tagline: m.blurb,
158	      taglineZh: m.blurbZh,
159	      description: m.blurb,
160	      descriptionZh: m.blurbZh,
161	      readmeMd: `# ${name}\n\nMCP server for **${name}** — ${m.blurb}.\n\n## Install\n\n\`\`\`bash\nagentcenter install ${m.slug}\n\`\`\`\n`,
162	      publishedAt: new Date(),
163	    }
164	  })
165	  console.log(`seed-mcp: upserting ${mcpExtRows.length} marketplace stubs`)
166	  if (mcpExtRows.length > 0) {
167	    await db
168	      .insert(extensions)
169	      .values(mcpExtRows)
170	      .onConflictDoUpdate({
171	        target: extensions.id,
172	        set: {
173	          slug: sql`excluded.slug`,
174	          category: sql`excluded.category`,
175	          scope: sql`excluded.scope`,
176	          ownerOrgId: sql`excluded.owner_org_id`,
177	          visibility: sql`excluded.visibility`,
178	          name: sql`excluded.name`,
179	          nameZh: sql`excluded.name_zh`,
180	          tagline: sql`excluded.tagline`,
181	          taglineZh: sql`excluded.tagline_zh`,
182	          description: sql`excluded.description`,
183	          descriptionZh: sql`excluded.description_zh`,
184	          readmeMd: sql`excluded.readme_md`,
185	          updatedAt: sql`now()`,
186	        },
187	      })
188	  }
189	
190	  // Landscape tool rows — the *product* headers. No per-MCP fields here.
191	  const toolRows = MCP_TOOLS.map((t) => {
192	    const parts = ownerToParts(t.owner)
193	    return {
194	      slug: t.slug,
195	      name: t.name,
196	      nameZh: t.nameZh ?? null,
197	      layer: parts.layer,
198	      ownerSector: parts.layer === "industry" ? parts.primary : null,
199	      ownerDomain: parts.layer === "public" ? parts.primary : null,
200	      ownerPdt:
201	        parts.layer === "public" && parts.secondary
202	          ? `${parts.primary}.${parts.secondary}`
203	          : null,
204	      blurb: t.blurb,
205	      blurbZh: t.blurbZh,
206	    }
207	  })
208	  console.log(`seed-mcp: upserting ${toolRows.length} landscape tools`)
209	  const upsertedTools = await db
210	    .insert(mcpLandscapeTools)
211	    .values(toolRows)
212	    .onConflictDoUpdate({
213	      target: mcpLandscapeTools.slug,
214	      set: {
215	        name: sql`excluded.name`,
216	        nameZh: sql`excluded.name_zh`,
217	        layer: sql`excluded.layer`,
218	        ownerSector: sql`excluded.owner_sector`,
219	        ownerDomain: sql`excluded.owner_domain`,
220	        ownerPdt: sql`excluded.owner_pdt`,
221	        blurb: sql`excluded.blurb`,
222	        blurbZh: sql`excluded.blurb_zh`,
223	        updatedAt: sql`now()`,
224	      },
225	    })
226	    .returning({ id: mcpLandscapeTools.id, slug: mcpLandscapeTools.slug })
227	
228	  const toolIdBySlug = new Map(upsertedTools.map((r) => [r.slug, r.id]))
229	
230	  // Per-MCP rows. Each MCP belongs to exactly one tool (via toolId) and
231	  // carries the per-MCP status fields, deps, tags, and marketplace link.
232	  const mcpRows = MCP_TOOLS.flatMap((t) => {
233	    const toolId = toolIdBySlug.get(t.slug)
234	    if (toolId === undefined) {
235	      throw new Error(`seed-mcp: missing tool id for slug "${t.slug}"`)
236	    }
237	    return t.mcps.map((m, i) => ({
238	      toolId,
239	      slug: m.slug,
240	      name: m.name ?? m.slug,
241	      nameZh: m.nameZh ?? null,
242	      extensionId: m.released ? `mcp-${m.slug}` : null,
243	      inDev: m.inDev,
244	      depsCount: m.depsCount,
245	      blurb: m.blurb,
246	      blurbZh: m.blurbZh,
247	      tags: m.tags,
248	      sortOrder: i,
249	    }))
250	  })
251	  // Prune MCPs whose slug is no longer in the canonical inventory — covers
252	  // the case where the seed renames or removes an MCP between deploys. We
253	  // do this BEFORE the upsert so the FK from extensions stays consistent;
254	  // mcps with extensionId set to a stub we're about to drop will null out
255	  // via the existing `onDelete: 'set null'` rule on the FK.
256	  const desiredMcpSlugs = mcpRows.map((r) => r.slug)
257	  if (desiredMcpSlugs.length > 0) {
258	    const stale = await db
259	      .delete(mcpLandscapeMcps)
260	      .where(notInArray(mcpLandscapeMcps.slug, desiredMcpSlugs))
261	      .returning({ slug: mcpLandscapeMcps.slug })
262	    if (stale.length > 0) {
263	      console.log(`seed-mcp: pruned ${stale.length} stale MCPs (${stale.map((s) => s.slug).join(", ")})`)
264	    }
265	  }
266	
267	  // Same cleanup for marketplace extension stubs the seed owns
268	  // (id prefix `mcp-`). Extensions never seeded by this file (real publisher
269	  // listings) keep their own ids and are untouched.
270	  const desiredExtIds = mcpExtRows.map((r) => r.id)
271	  const staleExts = await db
272	    .delete(extensions)
273	    .where(and(
274	      sql`${extensions.id} LIKE 'mcp-%'`,
275	      desiredExtIds.length > 0
276	        ? notInArray(extensions.id, desiredExtIds)
277	        : sql`true`,
278	    ))
279	    .returning({ id: extensions.id })
280	  if (staleExts.length > 0) {
281	    console.log(`seed-mcp: pruned ${staleExts.length} stale extension stubs (${staleExts.map((s) => s.id).join(", ")})`)
282	  }
283	
284	  console.log(`seed-mcp: upserting ${mcpRows.length} landscape MCPs`)
285	  if (mcpRows.length > 0) {
286	    await db
287	      .insert(mcpLandscapeMcps)
288	      .values(mcpRows)
289	      .onConflictDoUpdate({
290	        target: mcpLandscapeMcps.slug,
291	        set: {
292	          toolId: sql`excluded.tool_id`,
293	          name: sql`excluded.name`,
294	          nameZh: sql`excluded.name_zh`,
295	          extensionId: sql`excluded.extension_id`,
296	          inDev: sql`excluded.in_dev`,
297	          depsCount: sql`excluded.deps_count`,
298	          blurb: sql`excluded.blurb`,
299	          blurbZh: sql`excluded.blurb_zh`,
300	          tags: sql`excluded.tags`,
301	          sortOrder: sql`excluded.sort_order`,
302	          updatedAt: sql`now()`,
303	        },
304	      })
305	  }
306	}
307	
308	// ─── Standalone entry-point: `bun scripts/seed-mcp-landscape.ts` ──────────────
309	async function main() {
310	  const url = process.env.DATABASE_URL
311	  if (!url) {
312	    console.error("seed-mcp: DATABASE_URL is not set")
313	    process.exit(1)
314	  }
315	  const client = postgres(url)
316	  const db = drizzle(client, { schema, casing: "snake_case" })
317	
318	  console.log("seed-mcp: starting")
319	  await seedMcpLandscape(db)
320	  console.log("seed-mcp: done")
321	  await client.end()
322	}
323	
324	// Bun-friendly check for "is this the entry script?" — true when invoked
325	// directly via `bun scripts/seed-mcp-landscape.ts`, false when imported.
326	// `Bun.main` and `import.meta.url` both work; pick the standard one.
327	const isEntry = import.meta.url === `file://${process.argv[1]}`
328	if (isEntry) {
329	  main()
330	    .then(() => process.exit(0))
331	    .catch((err) => {
332	      console.error("seed-mcp: failed")
333	      console.error(err)
334	      process.exit(1)
335	    })
336	}
337	
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/scripts/seed-catalog.ts",
  "content": "// Idempotent catalog seed.\n//\n// Safe to run on every deploy. Mirrors scripts/seed-mcp-landscape.ts in\n// strategy: upserts a fixed inventory of extensions (id prefix `cat-`) plus\n// the small set of supporting rows it needs (default org, tag vocabulary),\n// then prunes any catalog-prefixed rows no longer in the inventory. Real\n// user-published extensions (any non-`cat-` id) are never touched.\n//\n// Wired into the Vercel build via `vercel-build` so a freshly deployed\n// environment carries a populated /extensions catalog.\n\nimport { and, inArray, notInArray, sql } from \"drizzle-orm\"\nimport { drizzle, type PostgresJsDatabase } from \"drizzle-orm/postgres-js\"\nimport postgres from \"postgres\"\n\nimport { CATALOG, type CatalogEntry } from \"../shared/data/catalog\"\nimport * as schema from \"../shared/db/schema\"\nimport {\n  extensions,\n  extensionTags,\n  organizations,\n  tags,\n} from \"../shared/db/schema\"\nimport { TAG_LABELS } from \"../shared/tags\"\n\ntype Db = PostgresJsDatabase<typeof schema>\n\nconst SYSTEM_ORG_ID = \"default\"\nconst CATALOG_ID_PREFIX = \"cat-\"\n\nfunction extensionIdFor(entry: CatalogEntry): string {\n  return `${CATALOG_ID_PREFIX}${entry.slug}`\n}\n\n/**\n * Upserts the catalog into `extensions` + `extension_tags`. Re-runnable;\n * never destructive to user data. Steps:\n *\n *   1. Ensure SYSTEM_ORG_ID exists (the owner for catalog rows).\n *   2. Upsert every tag from TAG_LABELS so referenced ids resolve in prod.\n *   3. Upsert each catalog entry by extension id.\n *   4. Replace tag links for each upserted extension (delete-then-insert\n *      scoped by extensionId — surgical, only the catalog row is touched).\n *   5. Prune extensions with id LIKE 'cat-%' not in the current CATALOG.\n */\nexport async function seedCatalog(db: Db): Promise<void> {\n  // 1. Owner org — same constant as seed-mcp-landscape; safe to upsert again.\n  await db\n    .insert(organizations)\n    .values({\n      id: SYSTEM_ORG_ID,\n      slug: SYSTEM_ORG_ID,\n      name: \"Default Organization\",\n      nameZh: \"默认组织\",\n    })\n    .onConflictDoNothing({ target: organizations.id })\n\n  // 2. Tag vocabulary. seed.ts seeds these in dev; in prod nothing else does,\n  //    so we upsert here too. onConflictDoNothing keeps existing rows verbatim\n  //    in case admin curation has edited a label by hand.\n  const tagRows = Object.entries(TAG_LABELS).map(([id, label]) => ({\n    id,\n    labelEn: label.en,\n    labelZh: label.zh,\n  }))\n  await db.insert(tags).values(tagRows).onConflictDoNothing()\n  console.log(`seed-catalog: ensured ${tagRows.length} tags`)\n\n  // 3. Extension upserts. Catalog rows are visibility=published with a fixed\n  //    publishedAt (so re-runs don't shift sort order); ownerOrgId is the\n  //    SYSTEM_ORG; publisherUserId is null (the schema allows it). deptId\n  //    stays null — catalog rows are department-agnostic.\n  const now = new Date()\n  const extRows = CATALOG.map((entry) => ({\n    id: extensionIdFor(entry),\n    slug: entry.slug,\n    category: entry.category,\n    scope: entry.scope,\n    funcCat: entry.funcCat,\n    subCat: entry.subCat,\n    l2: entry.l2,\n    badge: entry.badge ?? null,\n    publisherUserId: null,\n    ownerOrgId: SYSTEM_ORG_ID,\n    deptId: null,\n    iconEmoji: entry.iconEmoji,\n    iconColor: entry.iconColor,\n    visibility: \"published\" as const,\n    name: entry.name,\n    nameZh: entry.nameZh,\n    tagline: entry.tagline,\n    taglineZh: entry.taglineZh,\n    description: entry.description,\n    descriptionZh: entry.descriptionZh,\n    downloadsCount: entry.downloadsCount,\n    starsAvg: entry.starsAvg,\n    ratingsCount: Math.floor(entry.downloadsCount / 50),\n    publishedAt: now,\n  }))\n\n  await db\n    .insert(extensions)\n    .values(extRows)\n    .onConflictDoUpdate({\n      target: extensions.id,\n      set: {\n        slug: sql`excluded.slug`,\n        category: sql`excluded.category`,\n        scope: sql`excluded.scope`,\n        funcCat: sql`excluded.func_cat`,\n        subCat: sql`excluded.sub_cat`,\n        l2: sql`excluded.l2`,\n        badge: sql`excluded.badge`,\n        ownerOrgId: sql`excluded.owner_org_id`,\n        iconEmoji: sql`excluded.icon_emoji`,\n        iconColor: sql`excluded.icon_color`,\n        visibility: sql`excluded.visibility`,\n        name: sql`excluded.name`,\n        nameZh: sql`excluded.name_zh`,\n        tagline: sql`excluded.tagline`,\n        taglineZh: sql`excluded.tagline_zh`,\n        description: sql`excluded.description`,\n        descriptionZh: sql`excluded.description_zh`,\n        downloadsCount: sql`excluded.downloads_count`,\n        starsAvg: sql`excluded.stars_avg`,\n        ratingsCount: sql`excluded.ratings_count`,\n        updatedAt: sql`now()`,\n      },\n    })\n  console.log(`seed-catalog: upserted ${extRows.length} extensions`)\n\n  // 4. Tag links — replace per-extension to keep edits in sync with the data\n  //    file. Scoped delete + insert is safe: it never touches other rows.\n  const catalogExtIds = extRows.map((r) => r.id)\n  await db\n    .delete(extensionTags)\n    .where(inArray(extensionTags.extensionId, catalogExtIds))\n  const tagLinkRows = CATALOG.flatMap((entry) =>\n    entry.tags.map((tagId) => ({\n      extensionId: extensionIdFor(entry),\n      tagId,\n    })),\n  )\n  if (tagLinkRows.length > 0) {\n    await db\n      .insert(extensionTags)\n      .values(tagLinkRows)\n      .onConflictDoNothing()\n  }\n  console.log(`seed-catalog: wired ${tagLinkRows.length} tag links`)\n\n  // 5. Prune catalog rows whose slug has been removed from CATALOG. Bound\n  //    by the `cat-` id prefix so non-catalog extensions are never affected.\n  const desiredIds = catalogExtIds\n  const stale = await db\n    .delete(extensions)\n    .where(\n      and(\n        sql`${extensions.id} LIKE ${`${CATALOG_ID_PREFIX}%`}`,\n        desiredIds.length > 0\n          ? notInArray(extensions.id, desiredIds)\n          : sql`true`,\n      ),\n    )\n    .returning({ id: extensions.id })\n  if (stale.length > 0) {\n    console.log(`seed-catalog: pruned ${stale.length} stale entries (${stale.map((s) => s.id).join(\", \")})`)\n  } else {\n    console.log(`seed-catalog: no stale entries to prune`)\n  }\n}\n\n// ─── Standalone entry-point: `bun scripts/seed-catalog.ts` ────────────────────\nasync function main() {\n  const url = process.env.DATABASE_URL\n  if (!url) {\n    console.error(\"seed-catalog: DATABASE_URL is not set\")\n    process.exit(1)\n  }\n  const client = postgres(url)\n  const db = drizzle(client, { schema, casing: \"snake_case\" })\n\n  console.log(\"seed-catalog: starting\")\n  await seedCatalog(db)\n  console.log(\"seed-catalog: done\")\n  await client.end()\n}\n\nconst isEntry = import.meta.url === `file://${process.argv[1]}`\nif (isEntry) {\n  main()\n    .then(() => process.exit(0))\n    .catch((err) => {\n      console.error(\"seed-catalog: failed\")\n      console.error(err)\n      process.exit(1)\n    })\n}\n"
}
```

```
113	export async function countFiltered(
114	  db: Transactable,
115	  filters: Filters,
116	  userDeptId?: string,
117	): Promise<number> {
118	  const where = buildExtensionWhere(db, filters, userDeptId)

126	export async function findFeatured(db: Transactable) {
127	  // Prefer the hand-curated row. Fall back to top-downloaded so the home
128	  // hero is never empty.
129	  //
130	  // Both queries cap to one row; without a stable tiebreaker the choice
131	  // would flap on ties (two curated rows published at the same instant,
132	  // or two extensions with identical download counts). Sort by id last so
133	  // the home hero is deterministic across requests.
134	  const [curated] = await db
135	    .select(listSelect)
136	    .from(extensions)
137	    .leftJoin(extensionTags, eq(extensionTags.extensionId, extensions.id))
138	    .where(
139	      and(
140	        eq(extensions.visibility, "published"),
141	        eq(extensions.featured, true),
142	      ),
143	    )
144	    .groupBy(extensions.id)
145	    .orderBy(desc(extensions.publishedAt), desc(extensions.id))
146	    .limit(1)
147	  if (curated) return curated
148	
149	  const [fallback] = await db
150	    .select(listSelect)
151	    .from(extensions)
152	    .leftJoin(extensionTags, eq(extensionTags.extensionId, extensions.id))
153	    .where(eq(extensions.visibility, "published"))
154	    .groupBy(extensions.id)
155	    .orderBy(
156	      desc(extensions.downloadsCount),
157	      desc(extensions.publishedAt),
158	      desc(extensions.id),
159	    )
160	    .limit(1)
161	  return fallback ?? null
162	}
163	
164	export async function findRelated(
165	  db: Transactable,
166	  extensionId: string,
167	  category: "skills" | "mcp" | "slash" | "plugins",
168	) {
169	  return db
170	    .select(relatedSelect)
171	    .from(extensions)
172	    .where(
173	      and(
174	        eq(extensions.category, category),
175	        eq(extensions.visibility, "published"),
176	        ne(extensions.id, extensionId),
177	      ),
178	    )
179	    .orderBy(desc(extensions.downloadsCount))
180	    .limit(4)
181	}
```

```
9	const ALL_DEPTS = "__all"
10	
11	// Driver-agnostic: matches postgres-js, PGlite, and PgTransaction so the
12	// query builder composes with the repository layer's `Transactable`.
13	type Db = PgDatabase<
14	  PgQueryResultHKT,
15	  typeof schema,
16	  ExtractTablesWithRelations<typeof schema>
17	>
18	
19	export function buildExtensionWhere(
20	  db: Db,
21	  filters: Filters,
22	  fallbackDeptId?: string,
23	): SQL | undefined {
24	  const clauses = [
25	    eq(extensions.visibility, "published"),
26	
27	    filters.category ? eq(extensions.category, filters.category) : undefined,
28	    filters.scope ? eq(extensions.scope, filters.scope) : undefined,
29	    filters.funcCat ? eq(extensions.funcCat, filters.funcCat) : undefined,
30	    filters.subCat ? eq(extensions.subCat, filters.subCat) : undefined,
31	    filters.l2 ? eq(extensions.l2, filters.l2) : undefined,
32	
33	    filters.creator
34	      ? eq(extensions.publisherUserId, filters.creator)
35	      : undefined,
36	    filters.publisher
37	      ? eq(extensions.ownerOrgId, filters.publisher)
38	      : undefined,
39	
40	    (() => {
41	      const dept = filters.dept ?? fallbackDeptId ?? MY_DEPT_ID
42	      if (dept === ALL_DEPTS) return undefined
43	      return or(
44	        eq(extensions.deptId, dept),
45	        like(extensions.deptId, `${dept}.%`),
46	      )
47	    })(),
48	
49	    filters.filter === "trending"
50	      ? sql`${extensions.downloadsCount} > 50000`
51	      : undefined,
52	    filters.filter === "new" ? eq(extensions.badge, "new") : undefined,
53	    filters.filter === "official"
54	      ? eq(extensions.badge, "official")
55	      : undefined,
56	    filters.filter === "free"
57	      ? sql`${extensions.licenseSpdx} IS NOT NULL`
58	      : undefined,
59	
60	    filters.q
61	      ? or(
62	          sql`${extensions.searchVector} @@ websearch_to_tsquery('simple', ${filters.q})`,
63	          ilike(extensions.name, `%${filters.q}%`),
64	          ilike(extensions.nameZh, `%${filters.q}%`),
65	        )
66	      : undefined,
67	
68	    filters.tags && filters.tags.length > 0
69	      ? inArray(
70	          extensions.id,
71	          db
72	            .select({ id: extensionTags.extensionId })
73	            .from(extensionTags)
74	            .where(inArray(extensionTags.tagId, filters.tags))
75	            .groupBy(extensionTags.extensionId)
76	            .having(
77	              filters.tagMatch === "all"
78	                ? sql`count(*) = ${filters.tags.length}`
79	                : sql`count(*) >= 1`,
80	            ),
81	        )
82	      : undefined,
83	  ].filter((c): c is SQL => Boolean(c))
84	
85	  return clauses.length > 0 ? and(...clauses) : undefined
86	}
87	
88	export function buildExtensionOrder(sort: Filters["sort"]) {
89	  switch (sort) {
90	    case "stars":
91	      return [desc(extensions.starsAvg), desc(extensions.downloadsCount)]
92	    case "recent":
93	      return [desc(extensions.publishedAt), desc(extensions.createdAt)]
94	    case "downloads":
95	    default:
96	      return [desc(extensions.downloadsCount)]
97	  }
98	}
99	
```

> AGENT

The dept filter (line 40-47 of `shared/search/query.ts`) defaults to `MY_DEPT_ID = "eng.cloud.infra"` and only matches extensions inside that subtree. My catalog rows all have `deptId: null` (catalog is intentionally department-agnostic), so the SQL `eq(deptId, ...)` + `LIKE` clauses don't match — they're filtered out. The 2 skills you see are the ones `seed.ts` happened to assign to `eng.cloud.infra` or descendants.

The right fix is to treat `deptId IS NULL` as "system-scoped, visible across all departments." Same fix benefits the `seed-mcp-landscape` stubs (also null deptId).

> AGENT

Fixed. The seeder was working — the rows are in the DB. What was hiding them was a separate bug in the dept filter: `MY_DEPT_ID` was used as the silent default and the SQL `OR` only included `eq(deptId, dept)` and `LIKE 'dept.%'`, both of which return `NULL` (not true) for null deptIds. Catalog + mcp-landscape entries were getting filtered out for anyone on a real dept.

**The fix** — one extra branch in `shared/search/query.ts`:
```ts
or(
  eq(extensions.deptId, dept),
  like(extensions.deptId, `${dept}.%`),
  sql`${extensions.deptId} IS NULL`,   // ← new
)
```

Refresh `/extensions?category=skills` in dev and you should see all 54 catalog skills plus the 6 from `seed.ts` (60 total). Test suite is 243/243 green; branch is now 5 unpushed commits ahead of origin.

> DEVELOPER

I don't like the vertical scrollbar in the extension list page, what do you think?

> AGENT

Sharing my read before touching code:

The browse layout pins the page to `h-screen` and puts the scroll on `<main class="overflow-y-auto">`. That gives you a **nested scroll container** — scrollbar appears *inside* the layout, not at the browser edge. On macOS-with-persistent-scrollbars it also reserves a gutter that shifts the grid by ~15 px when results spill past one screen. That's the weight you're feeling.

**My recommendation: native body scroll, sticky sidebar.** Remove `h-screen` + nested `overflow-y-auto`; let the page scroll naturally with the browser's scrollbar at the viewport edge. Make `<aside>` `sticky top-16` so it stays in view as content scrolls. The header is already sticky. This is how GitHub / Linear / most modern marketplaces work — feels considerably lighter and avoids the column-shift on macOS.

Same fix applies to `default.vue` (also nested-scroll). `mcp-panorama` deliberately wants a viewport-locked split, so leave it alone.

Let me make the change.

> AGENT

Done in one commit. Stack is now 6 ahead of `origin/refactor/layout-shell`.

**What changed:**
- `app/layouts/browse.vue` + `app/layouts/default.vue`: `h-screen` → `min-h-screen`, dropped `overflow-y-auto` on `<main>`. Browse's `<aside>` becomes `sticky top-16 self-start h-[calc(100vh-4rem)]` so it pins below the header as the page scrolls.
- Page now scrolls with the browser's native scrollbar at the viewport edge. No nested scroll container, no macOS column-shift when results overflow.
- `mcp-panorama.vue` untouched — its split needs viewport locking.

Reload `/extensions` — the scrollbar should now sit at the browser edge instead of inside the content column, and the grid won't shift when results pass one screen.

and I think we do not need the hide/show sidebar button in the nav bar, what do you think?

> AGENT

Agreed — and the data confirms it.

**My take:** Remove it.

- It only ever appeared on `/extensions` (the `noSidebar` flag suppresses it everywhere else). And on mcp-panorama the state is wired up but **nothing reads it** — it was a UI ghost.
- The sidebar is 240 px of light Categories nav, not a heavy panel that earns its own collapse control. The cost (one more button in the navbar, persistent `useState`, discoverability problem if a user collapses it and forgets how to bring it back) outweighs the benefit.
- For narrow viewports the right answer is a media-query hide (`md:flex` on the aside), not a manual toggle. Editorial-Ivory wants fewer controls.

Making it so: drop the hamburger + the `collapsed`/`noSidebar` props from TopBar, drop the collapse state from browse + mcp-panorama, hide the sidebar below `md`.

Tag links — replace per-extension to keep edits in sync with the data\n+  //    file. Scoped delete + insert is safe: it never touches other rows.\n+  const catalogExtIds = extRows.map((r) => r.id)\n+  await db\n+    .delete(extensionTags)\n+    .where(inArray(extensionTags.extensionId, catalogExtIds))\n+  const tagLinkRows = CATALOG.flatMap((entry) =>\n+    entry.tags.map((tagId) => ({\n+      extensionId: extensionIdFor(entry),\n+      tagId,\n+    })),\n+  )\n+  if (tagLinkRows.length > 0) {\n+    await db\n+      .insert(extensionTags)\n+      .values(tagLinkRows)\n+      .onConflictDoNothing()\n+  }\n+  console.log(`seed-catalog: wired ${tagLinkRows.length} tag links`)\n+\n+  // 5. Fix only still-valid issues, skip the\nrest with a brief reason, keep changes minimal, and validate.\n\nIn `@scripts/seed-catalog.ts` around lines 46 - 171, seedCatalog runs many\ndependent DB mutations without a transaction; wrap the entire function body in a\nsingle DB transaction so either all steps succeed or none are applied.\nSpecifically, open a transaction via db.transaction(async (tx) => { ... }) (or\nthe project’s equivalent), then replace every use of db inside seedCatalog (the\ninsert/upsert calls for organizations/tags/extensions, the delete/insert on\nextensionTags, and the prune delete) with tx so all statements (the logic around\nextensionIdFor, extRows, tagLinkRows, and the returning of stale rows) execute\nwithin the same transactional callback; ensure the transaction is awaited so it\ncommits or rolls back on error.\n```\n\n</details>\n\n<!-- fingerprinting:phantom:poseidon:hawk -->\n\n<!-- This is an auto-generated comment by CodeRabbit -->","created_at":"2026-05-23T02:28:55Z","updated_at":"2026-05-23T02:28:56Z","html_url":"https://github.com/ainaive/agentcenter-nuxt/pull/32#discussion_r3291938309","pull_request_url":"https://api.github.com/repos/ainaive/agentcenter-nuxt/pulls/32","_links":{"self":{"href":"https://api.github.com/repos/ainaive/agentcenter-nuxt/pulls/comments/3291938309"},"html":{"href":"https://github.com/ainaive/agentcenter-nuxt/pull/32#discussion_r3291938309"},"pull_request":{"href":"https://api.github.com/repos/ainaive/agentcenter-nuxt/pulls/32"}},"reactions":{"url":"https://api.github.com/repos/ainaive/agentcenter-nuxt/pulls/comments/3291938309/reactions","total_count":0,"+1":0,"-1":0,"laugh":0,"hooray":0,"confused":0,"heart":0,"rocket":0,"eyes":0},"start_line":46,"original_start_line":46,"start_side":"RIGHT","line":171,"original_line":171,"side":"RIGHT","author_association":"NONE","original_position":171,"position":171,"subject_type":"line"}]

> AGENT

CodeRabbit (the only reviewer so far) posted **3 findings**. CI is green: Vercel preview ✅, validate ✅. Summary:

### 1. Sidebar uses tinted fills for secondary active states (Major)

`app/components/layout/Sidebar.vue` lines 39-40, 49-50, 63-64, 83-84 — the active row uses `bg-(--color-sidebar)/60`. CLAUDE.md §11 reserves tinted fills / accent for primary CTAs; secondary active states (sidebar nav included) should use **neutral ink + left-rule or bold weight**.

This is **pre-existing** for the funcCat/l1/l2 rows (was there before this PR) but my new "All" row inherited the same pattern. Easy fix: swap `bg-(--color-sidebar)/60 text-(--color-ink)` for `text-(--color-ink) font-semibold` across the 4 active-state classes.

### 2. `<aside>` landmark missing accessible label (Minor)

`app/layouts/browse.vue` line 22-25 — `role="navigation"` without `aria-label`. The TopBar already has `aria-label="…nav.primary…"`, so we now have two nav landmarks and a screen reader can't distinguish them. One-line fix: add `:aria-label="t('sidebar.categories')"` to the aside.

### 3. `seedCatalog` not atomic (Major)

`scripts/seed-catalog.ts` performs org upsert → tags upsert → extensions upsert → `extensionTags` delete+insert → prune as 5 separate statements. A mid-run failure (e.g. between the `extensionTags` delete and insert) leaves catalog extensions with no tag links until the next deploy. Wrap the whole body in `db.transaction(async (tx) => …)`.

Note: `seed-mcp-landscape.ts` has the same shape and isn't wrapped either — but that's a separate scope; we'd want to do it consistently in a follow-up.

---

**My read:** all three are valid. The Sidebar one is a real CLAUDE.md violation and the cleanup also touches pre-existing code. The aria-label is genuinely missing. The transaction one is the cheapest correctness win available.

Want me to apply all three as one cleanup commit? Or pick a subset?
