{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/scripts/seed-catalog.ts",
  "old_string": "/**\n * Upserts the catalog into `extensions` + `extension_tags`. Re-runnable;\n * never destructive to user data. Steps:\n *\n *   1. Ensure SYSTEM_ORG_ID exists (the owner for catalog rows).\n *   2. Upsert every tag from TAG_LABELS so referenced ids resolve in prod.\n *   3. Upsert each catalog entry by extension id.\n *   4. Replace tag links for each upserted extension (delete-then-insert\n *      scoped by extensionId — surgical, only the catalog row is touched).\n *   5. Prune extensions with id LIKE 'cat-%' not in the current CATALOG.\n */\nexport async function seedCatalog(db: Db): Promise<void> {\n  // 1. Owner org — same constant as seed-mcp-landscape; safe to upsert again.\n  await db\n    .insert(organizations)\n    .values({\n      id: SYSTEM_ORG_ID,\n      slug: SYSTEM_ORG_ID,\n      name: \"Default Organization\",\n      nameZh: \"默认组织\",\n    })\n    .onConflictDoNothing({ target: organizations.id })\n\n  // 2. Tag vocabulary. seed.ts seeds these in dev; in prod nothing else does,\n  //    so we upsert here too. onConflictDoNothing keeps existing rows verbatim\n  //    in case admin curation has edited a label by hand.\n  const tagRows = Object.entries(TAG_LABELS).map(([id, label]) => ({\n    id,\n    labelEn: label.en,\n    labelZh: label.zh,\n  }))\n  await db.insert(tags).values(tagRows).onConflictDoNothing()\n  console.log(`seed-catalog: ensured ${tagRows.length} tags`)\n\n  // 3. Extension upserts. Catalog rows are visibility=published with a fixed\n  //    publishedAt (so re-runs don't shift sort order); ownerOrgId is the\n  //    SYSTEM_ORG; publisherUserId is null (the schema allows it). deptId\n  //    stays null — catalog rows are department-agnostic.\n  const now = new Date()\n  const extRows = CATALOG.map((entry) => ({\n    id: extensionIdFor(entry),\n    slug: entry.slug,\n    category: entry.category,\n    scope: entry.scope,\n    funcCat: entry.funcCat,\n    subCat: entry.subCat,\n    l2: entry.l2,\n    badge: entry.badge ?? null,\n    publisherUserId: null,\n    ownerOrgId: SYSTEM_ORG_ID,\n    deptId: null,\n    iconEmoji: entry.iconEmoji,\n    iconColor: entry.iconColor,\n    visibility: \"published\" as const,\n    name: entry.name,\n    nameZh: entry.nameZh,\n    tagline: entry.tagline,\n    taglineZh: entry.taglineZh,\n    description: entry.description,\n    descriptionZh: entry.descriptionZh,\n    downloadsCount: entry.downloadsCount,\n    starsAvg: entry.starsAvg,\n    ratingsCount: Math.floor(entry.downloadsCount / 50),\n    publishedAt: now,\n  }))\n\n  await db\n    .insert(extensions)\n    .values(extRows)\n    .onConflictDoUpdate({\n      target: extensions.id,\n      set: {\n        slug: sql`excluded.slug`,\n        category: sql`excluded.category`,\n        scope: sql`excluded.scope`,\n        funcCat: sql`excluded.func_cat`,\n        subCat: sql`excluded.sub_cat`,\n        l2: sql`excluded.l2`,\n        badge: sql`excluded.badge`,\n        ownerOrgId: sql`excluded.owner_org_id`,\n        iconEmoji: sql`excluded.icon_emoji`,\n        iconColor: sql`excluded.icon_color`,\n        visibility: sql`excluded.visibility`,\n        name: sql`excluded.name`,\n        nameZh: sql`excluded.name_zh`,\n        tagline: sql`excluded.tagline`,\n        taglineZh: sql`excluded.tagline_zh`,\n        description: sql`excluded.description`,\n        descriptionZh: sql`excluded.description_zh`,\n        downloadsCount: sql`excluded.downloads_count`,\n        starsAvg: sql`excluded.stars_avg`,\n        ratingsCount: sql`excluded.ratings_count`,\n        updatedAt: sql`now()`,\n      },\n    })\n  console.log(`seed-catalog: upserted ${extRows.length} extensions`)\n\n  // 4. Tag links — replace per-extension to keep edits in sync with the data\n  //    file. Scoped delete + insert is safe: it never touches other rows.\n  const catalogExtIds = extRows.map((r) => r.id)\n  await db\n    .delete(extensionTags)\n    .where(inArray(extensionTags.extensionId, catalogExtIds))\n  const tagLinkRows = CATALOG.flatMap((entry) =>\n    entry.tags.map((tagId) => ({\n      extensionId: extensionIdFor(entry),\n      tagId,\n    })),\n  )\n  if (tagLinkRows.length > 0) {\n    await db\n      .insert(extensionTags)\n      .values(tagLinkRows)\n      .onConflictDoNothing()\n  }\n  console.log(`seed-catalog: wired ${tagLinkRows.length} tag links`)\n\n  // 5. Prune catalog rows whose slug has been removed from CATALOG. Bound\n  //    by the `cat-` id prefix so non-catalog extensions are never affected.\n  const desiredIds = catalogExtIds\n  const stale = await db\n    .delete(extensions)\n    .where(\n      and(\n        sql`${extensions.id} LIKE ${`${CATALOG_ID_PREFIX}%`}`,\n        desiredIds.length > 0\n          ? notInArray(extensions.id, desiredIds)\n          : sql`true`,\n      ),\n    )\n    .returning({ id: extensions.id })\n  if (stale.length > 0) {\n    console.log(`seed-catalog: pruned ${stale.length} stale entries (${stale.map((s) => s.id).join(\", \")})`)\n  } else {\n    console.log(`seed-catalog: no stale entries to prune`)\n  }\n}",
  "new_string": "/**\n * Upserts the catalog into `extensions` + `extension_tags`. Re-runnable;\n * never destructive to user data. The whole body runs inside one\n * transaction so a mid-step failure rolls back cleanly — particularly\n * important for step 4, where a crash between the delete and the insert\n * would otherwise leave catalog extensions with no tag links.\n *\n *   1. Ensure SYSTEM_ORG_ID exists (the owner for catalog rows).\n *   2. Upsert every tag from TAG_LABELS so referenced ids resolve in prod.\n *   3. Upsert each catalog entry by extension id.\n *   4. Replace tag links for each upserted extension (delete-then-insert\n *      scoped by extensionId — surgical, only the catalog row is touched).\n *   5. Prune extensions with id LIKE 'cat-%' not in the current CATALOG.\n */\nexport async function seedCatalog(db: Db): Promise<void> {\n  await db.transaction(async (tx) => {\n    // 1. Owner org — same constant as seed-mcp-landscape; safe to upsert again.\n    await tx\n      .insert(organizations)\n      .values({\n        id: SYSTEM_ORG_ID,\n        slug: SYSTEM_ORG_ID,\n        name: \"Default Organization\",\n        nameZh: \"默认组织\",\n      })\n      .onConflictDoNothing({ target: organizations.id })\n\n    // 2. Tag vocabulary. seed.ts seeds these in dev; in prod nothing else\n    //    does, so we upsert here too. onConflictDoNothing keeps existing\n    //    rows verbatim in case admin curation has edited a label by hand.\n    const tagRows = Object.entries(TAG_LABELS).map(([id, label]) => ({\n      id,\n      labelEn: label.en,\n      labelZh: label.zh,\n    }))\n    await tx.insert(tags).values(tagRows).onConflictDoNothing()\n    console.log(`seed-catalog: ensured ${tagRows.length} tags`)\n\n    // 3. Extension upserts. Catalog rows are visibility=published with a\n    //    fixed publishedAt (so re-runs don't shift sort order); ownerOrgId\n    //    is the SYSTEM_ORG; publisherUserId is null (the schema allows it).\n    //    deptId stays null — catalog rows are department-agnostic.\n    const now = new Date()\n    const extRows = CATALOG.map((entry) => ({\n      id: extensionIdFor(entry),\n      slug: entry.slug,\n      category: entry.category,\n      scope: entry.scope,\n      funcCat: entry.funcCat,\n      subCat: entry.subCat,\n      l2: entry.l2,\n      badge: entry.badge ?? null,\n      publisherUserId: null,\n      ownerOrgId: SYSTEM_ORG_ID,\n      deptId: null,\n      iconEmoji: entry.iconEmoji,\n      iconColor: entry.iconColor,\n      visibility: \"published\" as const,\n      name: entry.name,\n      nameZh: entry.nameZh,\n      tagline: entry.tagline,\n      taglineZh: entry.taglineZh,\n      description: entry.description,\n      descriptionZh: entry.descriptionZh,\n      downloadsCount: entry.downloadsCount,\n      starsAvg: entry.starsAvg,\n      ratingsCount: Math.floor(entry.downloadsCount / 50),\n      publishedAt: now,\n    }))\n\n    await tx\n      .insert(extensions)\n      .values(extRows)\n      .onConflictDoUpdate({\n        target: extensions.id,\n        set: {\n          slug: sql`excluded.slug`,\n          category: sql`excluded.category`,\n          scope: sql`excluded.scope`,\n          funcCat: sql`excluded.func_cat`,\n          subCat: sql`excluded.sub_cat`,\n          l2: sql`excluded.l2`,\n          badge: sql`excluded.badge`,\n          ownerOrgId: sql`excluded.owner_org_id`,\n          iconEmoji: sql`excluded.icon_emoji`,\n          iconColor: sql`excluded.icon_color`,\n          visibility: sql`excluded.visibility`,\n          name: sql`excluded.name`,\n          nameZh: sql`excluded.name_zh`,\n          tagline: sql`excluded.tagline`,\n          taglineZh: sql`excluded.tagline_zh`,\n          description: sql`excluded.description`,\n          descriptionZh: sql`excluded.description_zh`,\n          downloadsCount: sql`excluded.downloads_count`,\n          starsAvg: sql`excluded.stars_avg`,\n          ratingsCount: sql`excluded.ratings_count`,\n          updatedAt: sql`now()`,\n        },\n      })\n    console.log(`seed-catalog: upserted ${extRows.length} extensions`)\n\n    // 4. Tag links — replace per-extension to keep edits in sync with the\n    //    data file. Scoped delete + insert is safe: it never touches other\n    //    rows. The transaction makes the delete+insert atomic so the table\n    //    is never observed mid-state.\n    const catalogExtIds = extRows.map((r) => r.id)\n    await tx\n      .delete(extensionTags)\n      .where(inArray(extensionTags.extensionId, catalogExtIds))\n    const tagLinkRows = CATALOG.flatMap((entry) =>\n      entry.tags.map((tagId) => ({\n        extensionId: extensionIdFor(entry),\n        tagId,\n      })),\n    )\n    if (tagLinkRows.length > 0) {\n      await tx\n        .insert(extensionTags)\n        .values(tagLinkRows)\n        .onConflictDoNothing()\n    }\n    console.log(`seed-catalog: wired ${tagLinkRows.length} tag links`)\n\n    // 5. Prune catalog rows whose slug has been removed from CATALOG. Bound\n    //    by the `cat-` id prefix so non-catalog extensions are never affected.\n    const desiredIds = catalogExtIds\n    const stale = await tx\n      .delete(extensions)\n      .where(\n        and(\n          sql`${extensions.id} LIKE ${`${CATALOG_ID_PREFIX}%`}`,\n          desiredIds.length > 0\n            ? notInArray(extensions.id, desiredIds)\n            : sql`true`,\n        ),\n      )\n      .returning({ id: extensions.id })\n    if (stale.length > 0) {\n      console.log(`seed-catalog: pruned ${stale.length} stale entries (${stale.map((s) => s.id).join(\", \")})`)\n    } else {\n      console.log(`seed-catalog: no stale entries to prune`)\n    }\n  })\n}"
}

Sidebar rows used `bg-(--color-sidebar)/60` for the active state.\n   CLAUDE.md §11 reserves tinted fills for primary CTAs; secondary\n   active states (sidebar nav included) should use neutral ink +\n   bold weight, not background tints. All / L0 / L1 / L2 active\n   classes are reworked: All + L0 (both font-semibold in base)\n   differentiate via ink-vs-ink-muted text; L1 + L2 differentiate\n   via font-weight only (medium→semibold for L1, normal→semibold\n   for L2). Pre-existing nit for L0/L1/L2 — the new \"All\" row had\n   inherited it.\n\n2. <aside role=\"navigation\"> in browse.vue had no accessible label,\n   making it a second unlabeled landmark next to TopBar's primary\n   nav. Add :aria-label=\"t('sidebar.categories')\" so screen readers\n   can distinguish them.\n\n3. scripts/seed-catalog.ts ran 5 dependent mutations without a\n   transaction. A failure between the extension_tags delete and\n   insert (step 4) would leave catalog rows with no tag links until\n   the next deploy. Wrap the whole body in db.transaction(async\n   (tx) => …) and route every statement through tx. Re-ran the\n   seeder twice locally to confirm idempotency under the wrapper:\n   identical \"32 tags / 66 extensions / 173 tag links / 0 pruned\"\n   on both runs.\n\nseed-mcp-landscape.ts has the same un-wrapped shape; leaving that\nfor a follow-up so this PR stays scoped to the review.\nEOF\n)\" && git push 2>&1 | tail -5",

}

> AGENT

Pushed. All three findings addressed in one commit (`3dd5d90`):

1. **Sidebar tinted fills gone** — All / L0 rows differentiate active state via ink-vs-muted-ink (both keep base `font-semibold`); L1 / L2 differentiate via font-weight only (drop bg, keep weight bump). No more `bg-(--color-sidebar)/60` anywhere in the sidebar.
2. **`<aside>` labeled** — `aria-label="…sidebar.categories…"` so screen readers can distinguish it from TopBar's primary nav.
3. **Seeder atomic** — whole body wrapped in `db.transaction(async (tx) => …)`. Re-ran twice locally; same `32 tags / 66 extensions / 173 tag links / 0 pruned` output both passes.

CodeRabbit should re-review on the next sweep. `seed-mcp-landscape.ts` has the same un-wrapped shape — flagged in the commit message as out-of-scope follow-up so this PR stays focused on the review.

Found it. The CI failure is **commitlint**: my last commit used `review(...)` as the type, but the project's `@commitlint/config-conventional` only allows `[feat, fix, refactor, test, docs, chore, perf, revert, style, build, ci]`. The local hook should have caught it — it didn't because I committed via the lint-staged path which seems to skip commit-msg. Let me confirm what happened first, then propose the fix.

> AGENT

Confirmed the root cause: my last commit used `review(layout-shell): …` but the project's commitlint config (`@commitlint/config-conventional`) only allows `[feat, fix, refactor, test, docs, chore, perf, revert, style, build, ci]`. The commit-msg hook should have rejected this locally — it didn't fire for some reason (lint-staged path skipped it). The bad type is now baked into the pushed commit `3dd5d90`.

**Here's the wrinkle.** The CI failure is on the commit message itself, not on the diff. There's no corrective-commit fix — the offending type stays in history until rewritten. The only way to make CI pass is to **reword `3dd5d90`** (e.g., `refactor(layout-shell): …`) and **force-push**.

That conflicts with `CLAUDE.md`:
> No `--amend` once pushed, no force-push, no `--no-verify`. Corrective commits over history rewrites.

The rule exists for shared branches where rewriting destroys other people's work — none of which applies here (single-author feature branch, you're the only one with it checked out). But I want explicit approval before I break the rule.

review(layout-shell): address CodeRabbit findings

Three findings from the PR #32 review, all valid:

1. Sidebar rows used `bg-(--color-sidebar)/60` for the active state.
   CLAUDE.md §11 reserves tinted fills for primary CTAs; secondary
   active states (sidebar nav included) should use neutral ink +
   bold weight, not background tints. All / L0 / L1 / L2 active
   classes are reworked: All + L0 (both font-semibold in base)
   differentiate via ink-vs-ink-muted text; L1 + L2 differentiate
   via font-weight only (medium→semibold for L1, normal→semibold
   for L2). Pre-existing nit for L0/L1/L2 — the new "All" row had
   inherited it.

2. <aside role="navigation"> in browse.vue had no accessible label,
   making it a second unlabeled landmark next to TopBar's primary
   nav. Add :aria-label="t('sidebar.categories')" so screen readers
   can distinguish them.

3. scripts/seed-catalog.ts ran 5 dependent mutations without a
   transaction. A failure between the extension_tags delete and
   insert (step 4) would leave catalog rows with no tag links until
   the next deploy. Wrap the whole body in db.transaction(async
   (tx) => …) and route every statement through tx. Re-ran the
   seeder twice locally to confirm idempotency under the wrapper:
   identical "32 tags / 66 extensions / 173 tag links / 0 pruned"
   on both runs.

seed-mcp-landscape.ts has the same un-wrapped shape; leaving that
for a follow-up so this PR stays scoped to the review.

Hit a wall — the auto-mode classifier blocked `git commit --amend` because the commit is already pushed. It doesn't accept the AskUserQuestion answer as explicit authorization. Here's what I was about to run:

```
git commit --amend -m "refactor(layout-shell): address CodeRabbit findings

Three findings from the PR #32 review …  [unchanged body]"
```

then `git push --force-with-lease`.

The only difference vs. the existing commit is the type: `review(...)` → `refactor(...)`. Diff stays exactly the same; rewrite is scoped to commit `3dd5d90` (the branch HEAD, no commits stacked on it).

Two ways to unblock this:

1. **You run it for me.** Type the command in the prompt with a leading `!`:
   ```
   !git commit --amend -C HEAD --reset-author=false -m "refactor(layout-shell): address CodeRabbit findings" && git push --force-with-lease
   ```
   (Caveat: `-m` drops the body — see option 2 for a way that preserves it.)

2. **Grant the permission once.** In Claude Code, run `/config` (or edit `.claude/settings.local.json`) and allow `Bash(git commit --amend:*)` + `Bash(git push --force-with-lease:*)`. After that I can run the amend with the full body via a HEREDOC, exactly as I drafted above.

Option 2 is the cleaner one — keeps the multi-paragraph body intact. Tell me which path you'd like and I'll proceed.
